# Appendix A — Capability inventory (generated 2026-10-23 from the audit workflow)

Every write handler in `src/app/api` plus server actions, grouped by cluster. Columns: key · route · roles · risk · approval · idempotent · AI coverage today · reuse readiness (direct = clean `(AuthUser, body)` service; wrapper = service needs NextRequest/FormData; inline = logic lives only in the route). Rows marked ⚑ were added by the adversarial verifier. Verifier corrections are folded into the notes column.


**Totals:** 700 write capabilities; AI reachable today: {'partial': 42, 'none': 658}.


## C1 Academic structure — 75 write capabilities

_Verifier: Nearly complete. All 42 route.ts files across the 13 dirs were checked, and every exported POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE (including sub-actions) is covered except one hidden capability: PATCH /api/subjects with classId (subject class move). No GET handler writes. No C1 write lives only in a server action beyond the ones already listed. 12 corrections, most important: bulk datesheet creates papers on exams.edit, model activation bypasses the GRADING policy lock, timetable.create is not empty, promote_students is not idempotent, and AI campus.create seeds no cycle or weekends._

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (37):** `GET /api/classes?campusId=&includeArchived=` (Resolve class name + section + academicYear to classId; incl); `GET /api/subjects?campusId=&classId=` (Resolve subject name within a class to subjectId and current); `GET /api/subjects/[subjectId]/syllabus` (List syllabus topics (ids, order, status) of a subject); `GET /api/academic/periods?campusId=&timeType=CLASS|EXAM` (Resolve period numbers/times to periodDefinitionId); `GET /api/academic/rooms?campusId=` (Resolve roomNumber to roomId with teaching/exam capacity and); `GET /api/academic/calendar?campusId=` (Effective weekend days, week start and holidays (holiday ids); `GET /api/academic/calendar/unified?campusId=&year=` (Unified academic calendar (holidays, exams, events); drafts ); `GET /api/academic-cycle?campusId=` (List academic cycles and the ACTIVE one (cycleId for transit); `GET /api/academic-cycle/closure?campusId=&year=` (Year closure readiness and blockers before closing or starti); `GET /api/academic-year?campusId=` (Classes grouped by academic year with status and counts); `GET /api/academic-year/history?campusId=&academicYear=&classId=&studentId=` (Student class history for a year/class/student); `GET /api/academic/rollover?campusId=&sourceYear=` (Rollover plan, version, per-learner decisions, blockers and ); `GET /api/academic/exam-sessions?campusId=&academicYear=` (Resolve exam session title/term to sessionId); `GET /api/academic/exam-sessions/[id]?campusId=` (One session with per-class exams and counts); `GET /api/academic/exam-schedule?examId=|sessionId=&campusId=` (Datesheet papers (scheduleId, subject, date, period, rooms) ); `GET /api/academic/exam-schedule/bulk?sessionId=` (Master datesheet planning grid with all conflicts); `GET /api/academic/exam-schedule/invigilation?sessionId=` (Invigilation duty roster with examRoomId and expectedHash ne); `GET /api/academic/exam-schedule/rooms?scheduleId=` (Seating plan for one paper); `GET /api/academic/exam-sessions/seating?campusId=&date=&periodDefinitionId=` (Campus seat supply and free rooms in a slot); `GET /api/academic/datesheet-publication?sessionId=` (Publication status, latest version (expectedVersion), pendin); `GET /api/academic/availability?campusId=&context=&day=&date=&periodDefinitionId=` (Free teachers/rooms for a timetable or exam slot); `GET /api/academic/impact?kind=&id=` (Dependency impact before deleting/archiving academic records); `GET /api/academic/hub-stats?campusId=&year=` (Academic setup counts (classes, subjects, teachers, timetabl); `GET /api/academic-models?campusId=&academicYear= | ?templates=1` (Model versions/classes per cohort; shared templates (SUPER_A); `GET /api/academic-models/[id]` (One academic model version); `GET /api/grade-config?classId=&academicYear= | ?scope=all&academicYear=` (Current grading rules per class and section drift); `GET /api/student-categories?campusId=` (Resolve category name to id with student counts); `GET /api/student-groups?campusId=` (Resolve group name to id with student counts); `GET /api/curriculum/map (no recordId) | ?recordId=&compareTo=` (List approved curricula; objective map between versions); `GET /api/timetable?campusId=&classId=&academicYear=` (Resolve class timetable to timetableId with slots (slotIds, ); `GET /api/timetable/[id] (?publication=1)` (One timetable with draftRevision; publication preview (confl); `GET /api/timetable/[id]/suggestions` (Conflicts and proposed fixes (SuggestionAction payloads)); `GET /api/timetable/teacher?teacherId=` (A teacher's weekly timetable (others' only for office roles)); `GET /api/timetable/class?classId=` (Class timetable for families/staff); `GET /api/timetable/substitute-duties?campusId=&date=` (Assigned substitute duties for a date (dutyIds)); `GET /api/cover` (My cover duties, lessons being covered and recoveries (dutyI); `GET /api/cover/campus?campusId=&date=` (Coordinator follow-up for a date (covered and uncovered less)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:finishOnboarding -> src/lib/onboarding/setup-service.ts:completeSetup: first-onboarding completion that creates/reactivates campuses (plan capacity check), upserts an ACTIVE AcademicCycle (campusId+academicYear, label, start/end dates) and creates Weekend rows per campus. There is no API route.
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:saveOnboardingStep -> src/lib/onboarding/setup-service.ts:saveSetup: saves questionnaire steps (academicYear, sessionLabel, weekends, campuses; src/lib/onboarding/setup-state.ts:78). Also assignOnboardingDelegate/revokeOnboardingDelegate (completeOnboarding.ts:147,162).
- src/app/actions/addCampus.ts:addCampus: group-console SUPER_ADMIN only (isGroupConsole). After creating a campus it seeds an ACTIVE AcademicCycle copied from a sibling campus and Weekend rows (default Sunday) (lines 130-166). Seeding errors are swallowed with console.error.
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:previewLocaleChange/applyLocaleChange/setLocaleDelegation (lines 78-140): scheduled locale-policy changes (weekend, timezone, etc.) that inspect existing exam schedules and take the timetable campus lock. These change the effective weekend that the calendar, exam scheduling and timetable slot validation read through getLocalePackage.
- src/lib/class-sections.ts:parseSections/expandSectionRange/parseSubjectList: client-side parsing (used by src/components/shared-admin/class-manager.tsx and quick-create-class.tsx) that expands section ranges such as 'A-D' and bulk subject lists before calling /api/classes and /api/subjects. It is a helper, not a server write.
- src/lib/cycleEvents.ts:emitCycleChanged: client-only browser event after cycle changes (used by src/components/academic-year/CycleManagementPanel.tsx). No server effect.

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Role predicates resolve to: canManageCampusOps = SUPER_ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, ADMIN (src/lib/api/scope.ts:230-232, isCampusOperator src/lib/auth/institution.ts:35); canManageOperations/canManageAcademics = those plus PRINCIPAL (scope.ts:219-227); canLeadAcademics = PRINCIPAL, or CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN on their own campus only while it has no active principal, never SUPER_ADMIN (scope.ts:249-258). Matrix defaults: SUPER_ADMIN fixed full; ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN full; PRINCIPAL has timetable and exams full, students/staff view only (src/lib/permissions.ts:195-221).
- AI coverage exists only through school.execute operations. schoolAiOperationsFor gives SUPER_ADMIN, TEACHER, LIBRARIAN and RECEPTIONIST no executable operations (src/lib/school-ai/access.ts:69-76). CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN get OPERATOR_TOOLS (setup.apply_changes, academics.setup_year, students.promote_enrollment...) plus ACADEMIC_TOOLS only while the campus has no principal; PRINCIPAL gets ACADEMIC_TOOLS (timetable.*, exams.create_session_plan, academics.transition_year, curriculum.import_topics) (access.ts:55-62,84-89). None of the direct catalog tools in src/lib/automation/catalog.ts touch this cluster.
- Seat divergences between REST and AI an adapter must resolve explicitly: (a) academic-cycle activate/pause/resume is campus-ops-only in REST (src/lib/school-ai/school-operations-cycle-write.ts:20) but academics.transition_year is a PRINCIPAL tool; (b) class-teacher assignment requires canLeadAcademics in REST (src/app/api/classes/route.ts:182) but setup.apply_changes TEACHER_ALLOCATIONS checks only exams.edit + timetable.edit for operators (src/lib/school-ai/school-setup-academics.ts:18-19); (c) academic model activation writes GradeWeightConfig with only canManageOperations (src/app/api/academic-models/[id]/route.ts:35,122) while /api/grade-config requires canLeadAcademics (src/app/api/grade-config/route.ts:220).
- The automation engine self-approves the workspace record it just created before executing (src/lib/automation/service.ts:79-81). Separation of duties applies only if the school's delivery policy requires it; the platform default is NOT_REQUIRED (src/lib/school-ai/access.ts:166-180). REST approval steps in this cluster (rollover approve, academic template approve, model activate) also do not enforce maker!=checker.
- Every scheduling-resource write (periods, rooms, holidays, weekends) must run inside withSchedulingResourceWrite: SERIALIZABLE schoolAiTransaction + lockTimetableCampus + live actor/accessVersion re-check + fresh matrix flag + assertSchoolOperational (src/lib/academic/resource-write.ts:10-22). Exam scheduling writes use the same fence through withExamSchedulingWrite (src/lib/academic/exam-scheduling-write.ts:9-26). Class/subject/timetable writes take lockTimetableCampus as well (src/lib/api/teacher-assignments.ts:50-53, src/lib/timetable/manual.ts:53-59). An adapter must call these wrappers, not raw prisma.
- Campus scoping: resolveCampusId pins non-SUPER_ADMIN users to their own campus and refuses a different requested campusId (src/lib/api/scope.ts:328-349); SUPER_ADMIN must name a campus. Several handlers load targets by id first via resourceScope/resolveExamSchedulingCampus/resolveTimetableCampus and then re-check inside the transaction.
- Academic year invariants: only one ACTIVE cycle per campus, earlier years must be ENDED before creating or activating a later one (school-operations-cycle-write.ts:30,37); unique campusId+academicYear (prisma/schema.prisma:5244); pausing or ending is blocked by in-progress online exam attempts and future substitute duties (cycle-write.ts:43-47); closing requires getYearClosureReport.canClose unless the academic lead forces it with a reason (cycle-write.ts:39-41). There are two different 'close year' paths: /api/academic-cycle end (cycle ENDED) and /api/academic-year close-year (classes COMPLETED + StudentClassHistory, no matrix check, src/app/api/academic-year/route.ts:51-169).
- Promotion has three paths: the reviewed rollover plan (prepare -> decide -> academic-lead approve -> registrar execute on effective date with destination capacity, src/lib/academic/rollover.ts:260-345), AI students.promote_enrollment (capacityConfirmed, reviewed preview), and legacy /api/academic-year promote/bulk-promote with no permission-matrix check, no capacity check and no campus comparison of student/target class (src/app/api/academic-year/route.ts:173-305). An adapter should not use the legacy path.
- Class invariants: normalized duplicate name+section+year per campus plus DB identity index (src/app/api/classes/route.ts:124-150, src/lib/academic/structure.ts findDuplicateClass); class teacher must be an active TEACHER or PRINCIPAL of the campus, subject teacher must be an active TEACHER (route:30-38, src/lib/academic/subject-write.ts:27); SINGLE teaching mode cascades the class teacher to every subject and timetable slot (teacher-assignments.ts:93-101); academicYear cannot change once timetables exist; archive is refused while active students remain (classes/route.ts:253) and is soft/restorable; section transfer stays within campus+year, is all-or-nothing and preserves enrollment history (src/app/api/classes/transfer/route.ts:22-23,60-85).
- Class.capacity is not written by POST/PATCH /api/classes even though classSchema parses capacity (src/lib/validators/schemas.ts:227-235); the only writer found in this cluster is the rollover 'capacity' action (src/lib/academic/rollover.ts:setClassCapacity). Questionnaire-driven setup that collects capacities needs that path or a new adapter.
- Subjects: delete refused when marks, exams or exam schedules reference it (teacher-assignments.ts:170-172); changing a scheduled subject's teacher needs timetable.edit (teacher-assignments.ts:37-41); applyToAllSections fans out to every ACTIVE section with the same class name and year (subject-write.ts:28).
- Timetables: one per class+year+term (schema.prisma:3128); publish is the academic lead's act, needs matching draftRevision and an effectiveFrom that is not in the past, and notifies teachers, students and guardians (src/app/api/timetable/[id]/route.ts:50-58, src/lib/timetable/manual.ts:159-176); deleting a timetable with substitute duties is refused (manual.ts:192); new slots must match campus period definitions and working days (src/lib/timetable/draft-slot.ts:59-65).
- Exams/datesheet: papers editable only while the exam is unlocked and DRAFT/ACTIVE/MARKS_ENTRY and inside the session window; single-room exam-spacing capacity enforced (src/app/api/academic/exam-schedule/route.ts:249-262); deleting a session never deletes exams with marks or released results (src/app/api/academic/exam-sessions/route.ts:151-172); datesheet publication is versioned (expectedVersion), blocked by hard issues, and needs a reason of 8 or more characters on republish (src/lib/academic/datesheet-publication.ts:142-154).
- Rooms and periods protect bookings: room layout changes are refused while exam seats exist and deletes while slots/papers use it (src/app/api/academic/rooms/route.ts:167,211-226); exam periods used by locked exams cannot shift, and periods used by datesheets cannot be deleted (src/app/api/academic/periods/route.ts:144,195). Period overlap within a timeType is rejected.
- Grading rules: weights must sum to 100 and grade thresholds must strictly descend; the write fans out to all ACTIVE sections of the class name; an approved group template can lock values (assertWritable 'GRADING', POLICY_LOCKED) and provenance is recorded (src/app/api/grade-config/route.ts:236-266,322-335). The route uses getAuthUser rather than requireAuthUser and turns non-policy errors into 500.
- Academic model templates: only SUPER_ADMIN creates/approves shared templates; campuses may adopt approved templates and override only delegated keys (src/app/api/academic-models/route.ts:63,107-111); activation refuses overlapping ACTIVE versions for the same classes and period (src/app/api/academic-models/[id]/route.ts:83-90,115-120).
- Cover/recovery (SKO-442) authority is record-relative: handover by the absent teacher or a campus leader, acknowledge only by the substitute, outcome once by the substitute or leader, recovery steps by the owner or leader with knownVersion locking; there are no permission-matrix checks (src/lib/cover/recovery.ts:40-140). recordUncovered requires the teacher to be on APPROVED leave and no assigned cover (recovery.ts:111-116).
- Side effects: many handlers send in-app notifications after commit (notify(...) for CLASS_CREATED/UPDATED/DELETED, SUBJECT_CREATED, SUBJECT_TEACHER_ASSIGNED, TIMETABLE_CREATED/PUBLISHED/DELETED, ACADEMIC_CYCLE_CHANGED, ACADEMIC_YEAR_CLOSED, STUDENTS_PROMOTED, EXAM_DATESHEET_PUBLISHED, COVER_HANDOVER_READY, LESSON_RECOVERY_NEEDED). These sit in route handlers, not services, so an adapter calling the service directly must reproduce or deliberately skip them, and a replayed success re-notifies.
- Idempotency: no handler in this cluster accepts an idempotency key. Replay safety comes from unique constraints (409), version/hash/revision preconditions, or absolute-set semantics. Replays duplicate rows for holiday create (no unique index, prisma/schema.prisma:1969-1980), syllabus topic create, exam session create (session row), academic model drafts and curriculum objective mappings. The automation engine's own receipt (school_ai_requests AUTOMATIC_ACTION_V1, src/lib/automation/role-action-service.ts:23-27) would have to wrap any new adapter.
- Several write routes lack a permission-matrix check and rely only on the role predicate: student-categories, student-groups, syllabus topics, class restore, academic-year POST, datesheet publish, timetable notify (see the gate of each row). An adapter should add assertWorkspacePermission explicitly rather than inherit these gaps.
- Input formats: invigilation PATCH requires same-origin (src/app/api/academic/exam-schedule/invigilation/route.ts:15); slot POST caps the body at 8KB; rollover/datesheet use inline zod schemas; most other handlers parse JSON by hand. An adapter needs its own zod schema per capability, following the existing strictObject proposals in src/lib/school-ai/*.ts.
- Onboarding questionnaire (flagship 2) already writes part of this cluster: completeSetup creates or reactivates campuses, upserts an ACTIVE AcademicCycle per campus and writes Weekend rows from the questionnaire (src/lib/onboarding/setup-service.ts:330-430), called by src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:finishOnboarding. Classes, subjects, periods, rooms, holidays and timetables are not created there; the dashboard keeps prompting 'Set up your school with AI' until active years, periods and weekends exist (src/app/actions/dashboard.ts:88-97).

## C2 People, staff and access — 61 write capabilities

_Verifier: Nearly complete. I checked 42 route.ts files (the 11 listed dirs plus /api/identity). Every exported POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE handler and every action branch is inventoried. The one write handler missing is the state-changing GET /api/auth/verify. The server-action list for this domain is complete. Two kinds of error need fixing. First, several scope claims ignore the Prisma guard's campus filter: leave type/allocation, department and hierarchy writes are limited to the operator's own campus. Second, the gates for invite, remove, resend and cancel are understated: the UI calls those server actions directly, which skips /api/staff's canManageCampusOps check._

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (22):** `GET /api/staff?campusId=&role=&includeInactive=` (Resolve CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN/PRINCIPAL/TEACHER names to user i); `GET /api/memberships` (All members with role, campus, isActive, delegation flags an); `GET /api/teachers/availability?campusId=` (Teachers and principals with specialties, led classes and we); `GET /api/staff/designations` (Rank ladder ids, levels and headcount (designationId for inv); `GET /api/staff/departments?campusId=` (Unit tree with heads and member rows (departmentId, memberId); `GET /api/staff/hierarchy?campusId=&includeFormer=` (Org chart nodes, solid/dotted edges); `GET /api/staff/appointments?userId=` (Service record, manager chain and secondary reporting lines ); `GET /api/staff/profile?userId=` (Payroll profile, documents and timeline (contains bank detai); `GET /api/staff/documents?userId=&documentId=` (List staff documents / private download); `GET /api/staff/workload?campusId=&weekStart=` (Per-teacher weekly load and capacity); `GET /api/staff/cover?campusId=&date=` (Uncovered lessons (slotId) and eligible candidates (teacherI); `GET /api/leave?mode=my|all|balances&campusId=&academicYear=&status=&userId=` (Own requests and balances; reviewer view of campus requests ); `GET /api/leave/types?campusId=` (Leave type ids by name for apply/allocations); `GET /api/leave/allocations?campusId=&academicYear=` (Allocation ids by type/role/user); `GET /api/teacher-attendance?campusId=&date=|month=|userId=` (Staff attendance register for a date / a person's month (tea); `GET /api/teachers/performance?campusId=&academicYear=&teacherId=` (Sourced teaching evidence (leaders: campus; teacher: self)); `GET /api/teachers/performance/notes?teacherId=&academicYear=&campusId=` (Support notes thread); `GET /api/roles/permissions` (Effective permission matrix per role/module; canEdit and mfa); `GET /api/identity?view=overview|candidates&id=|batch&id=` (Identity connections, pending link requests, roster batches ); `GET /api/profile` (Own profile); `GET /api/auth/sessions` (Own active sessions (ids for DELETE)); `GET /api/invite/status?token=` (Public invitation status/context by token)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- src/app/actions/invite.ts:acceptInvite / acceptInviteResult (l.270-296): invitee accepts invitation with password, creates/activates account (no API route)
- src/app/actions/auth/reset.ts:requestPasswordReset (l.20), verifyToken (l.38), resetPassword (l.45): password reset flow (no API route)
- src/app/actions/auth/logout.ts:logout (l.23): server-action sign-out alongside /api/auth/logout
- src/app/actions/completeTeacherOnboarding.ts:getTeacherOnboardingSession (l.44) / completeTeacherOnboarding (l.122): teacher first-login profile (fullName, phone, CNIC, DOB, gender, qualification, specialization, subjectSpecialties, teachesAllSubjects, address, emergency contacts, joiningDate, profile image); sets onboardingComplete and re-mints the JWT
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:getOnboardingSession (l.61), saveOnboardingStep (l.81), finishOnboarding (l.91), assignOnboardingDelegate (l.147), revokeOnboardingDelegate (l.162) -> src/lib/onboarding/setup-service.ts saveSetup/completeSetup/assignSetupDelegate/revokeSetupDelegate: institution setup wizard and setup co-owner account with temporary password (no API route; core of feature 2)
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:saveLanguagePreference (l.63): personal display language (automation profile.update covers preferredLanguage but not the language cookie)
- src/app/actions/invite.ts:inviteStaff/removeStaff/cancelInvitation/resendInvitation (l.91-268) are exported server actions callable directly from UI as well as via /api/staff

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Automation coverage today: the catalog (src/lib/automation/catalog.ts:27-56) offers no staff, invitation, membership, leave, staff-attendance, hierarchy or permission tool. Only profile.update (own user row, catalog.ts:29; role-action-service.ts:30-33) overlaps this cluster. Typed operations that touch it: setup.apply_changes action STAFF (bulk pending invitations) and timetable.assign_substitutes (executes cover proposals) (school-ai/execution.ts:36,43).
- setup.apply_changes STAFF is the only existing AI path for staffing. It is limited to TEACHER/ACCOUNTANT/LIBRARIAN/RECEPTIONIST rows (school-setup.ts:9) and max 30 rows (school-setup.ts:24). It needs a membership manager (school-setup-service.ts:84-85) and repeats the teacher allowance check. It writes invitations with deliveryStatus 'pending' and canManageMemberships=false, and sends no email (school-setup-service.ts:133); nextStep is STAFF_DELIVERY (:148). An onboarding questionnaire therefore needs a delivery adapter (resendInvitation-style) and a leader-appointment adapter. ROLE_SETUP_INTERVIEW also tells SUPER_ADMIN that campus admins are appointed through the access-management flow (automation/role-setup.ts:4).
- Membership authority (membership-access.ts:55-76). The institution authority is SUPER_ADMIN in a GROUP or ADMIN in a STANDALONE school (auth/institution.ts:24-27), decided by role and kind, not by the owner flag. A delegate holds canManageMemberships and works only on its own campus. A delegate cannot appoint CAMPUS_ADMIN/PRINCIPAL or grant delegations. Turning a delegation on needs mfaVerified===true (l.73, 108-109). Subscription purchasing is never delegated through an invitation (invitation-write.ts:146).
- Seat invariants: at most one PRINCIPAL per campus (active or invited). At most one CAMPUS_ADMIN per campus unless the institution authority adds a deputy (invitation-write.ts:117-128). These checks run only on invite. PUT /api/memberships role changes and roster applyBatch do not re-check them (memberships/route.ts:36-62; identity/service.ts:395-440 excerpt).
- Plan capacity: the teacher allowance counts active TEACHER users plus pending TEACHER invitations across all campuses, under a school row lock (invitation-write.ts:26-31, 36). An adapter must call createStaffInvitation (or the setup path) and never insert staff_invitations directly.
- Invitation identity rules: tenant-scoped email. An active account with the email returns 409. A pending invitation for the email returns 409. An inactive account is reusable only with the same campus and a compatible role (invitation-write.ts:63-80). Designation, department and manager ids are validated at invite time (l.86-115). Server-action rate limit is 20 admin invite actions per 5 min per actor (invite.ts:99,143,195,224), which bulk onboarding must respect.
- Deactivation versus HR status: removeStaff deactivates the login, bumps accessVersion and revokes sessions (invite.ts:167-171). setStaffPosition with RESIGNED/RETIRED/TERMINATED only closes department memberships and reporting lines; the account stays active (hierarchy.ts:528-538). An 'offboard' workflow needs both.
- No maker-checker on HR/payroll edits: setStaffPosition records approvedById = actor (hierarchy.ts:576). Salary changes go through /api/staff/hierarchy and /api/staff/profile with no second approver. Bank account details are written in /api/staff/profile. An AI adapter should exclude bank fields and treat salary as FINANCIAL needing human review (the platform's canApproveFinance checker seat exists at scope.ts:240-242 but is not used here).
- Leave separation of duties: teachers' leave is decided by the academic lead (canLeadAcademics). Everyone else's, the principal included, is decided by the campus operator (leave/review/route.ts:31-35). No guard stops a CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN from approving their own request; an adapter must add request.userId !== actor. Approval is refused on allocation overrun unless override=true (l.56-73). It is also refused when the person has ASSIGNED substitute duty or exam invigilation in range (l.48-49). Approval re-plans meetings (reconcileMeetings).
- Leave apply checks only isStaffRole (leave/route.ts:134-136), not the leave.add matrix bit (V2 defaults give ACCOUNTANT/LIBRARIAN/RECEPTIONIST/TEACHER leave.add true, permissions.ts:195-281). The overlap guard returns 409. Client-supplied 'days' overrides the computed range.
- Campus scope gaps an adapter must enforce itself. Leave type/allocation PATCH/DELETE look records up by school, not the actor's campus (leave/types/route.ts:80-82,110-113; leave/allocations/route.ts:97-99,123-125). Hierarchy, reporting-lines and department member/head writes find users/departments by id under the tenant guard only. /api/users PATCH has no role filter and no staff matrix check (users/route.ts:12-24). The teacher-attendance leader branch does not verify that entry.userId is a teacher on the campus, and it skips the attendance matrix (teacher-attendance/route.ts:189-215).
- Role permission matrix edits: only the institution authority, with a verified second factor (permission-administration.ts:18-35), may make them. Never the editor's own role or fixed roles. View=false clears add/edit/delete. A standalone ADMIN always keeps staff.view (roles/permissions/route.ts:80-96). APP_OWNER/SUPER_ADMIN are fixed full access (permissions.ts:285-292). A stored row equal to the V1 default and written before 2026-10-26 is read as the V2 default (permissions.ts:283, 308-314).
- Concurrency/idempotency patterns to preserve: memberships PUT requires reviewed:true and expectedVersion (CAS on accessVersion), and revokes the target's sessions. proposeCover requires an idempotencyKey (workload.ts:164-169). Invitation create/cancel/resend use status+token compare-and-set (invite.ts:211-215, 245-248). automation's executeRoleAction pattern (school row lock + schoolAiRequest receipt keyed by idempotencyKey, role-action-service.ts:23-28) is the template for new adapters, because most routes here have no key.
- Live-authority re-reads: createStaffInvitation and withSchedulingResourceWrite re-read the actor's role, campus and accessVersion inside the transaction. They refuse on drift (invitation-write.ts:37-39; resource-write.ts:14-15). The AutomaticJob.accessVersion snapshot (automation/schema.ts:32) should be checked against these.
- Credential and pre-auth flows must never be automated: login, signup-step1/2, MFA (/api/auth/mfa, /api/account/mfa), change/first password, SSO callback, invite reissue/accept, password reset. Note that GET /api/auth/verify mutates state: it activates a pending account from a signed token (auth/verify/route.ts:6-30).
- Onboarding (feature 2) building blocks in this cluster: applyInstitutionPreset seeds ranks and campus units idempotently by name (hierarchy.ts:76-140). Leave types and allocations can seed a default leave policy. createStaffInvitation provides typed staffing. assignSetupDelegate (onboarding/setup-service.ts:517) creates a co-owner SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN with a temporary password returned to the UI, and only the institution owner may call it. signup-step2 creates the owner with isActive=false until email verification and onboardingComplete=false (register.ts:147-161).
- Roster sync (identity applyBatch) bypasses invitations. It creates active staff users directly with the IdP-mapped role (MAPPABLE_ROLES include PRINCIPAL and CAMPUS_ADMIN, identity/service.ts:26). It deactivates withdrawn staff and revokes their sessions (l.434-437). The preview hash must be bound (l.357-358).
- Name resolution: /api/staff omits ACCOUNTANT/LIBRARIAN/RECEPTIONIST (staff/route.ts:7), so use /api/memberships or a dedicated lookup for office staff. assistant-catalog-service refuses ambiguous teacher names without a distinguishing code (assistant-catalog-service.ts:83). Reuse that rule.
- PII: CNIC, DOB, emergency contacts, bank details and staff documents are personal or financial identifiers. Pseudonymize before any prompt (project AI PII policy), and keep the values out of audit rows as the routes already do (users/route.ts:91-94; staff/profile/route.ts:149-156).
- Related writers outside this cluster's listed dirs (not covered here): src/app/api/super/users/[id]/password/route.ts (PUT), src/app/api/owner/users/route.ts and src/app/api/owner/users/[id]/password/route.ts (platform), and src/lib/students/create.ts:305,384 (guardian/student invitations created with student records).

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (33):** `GET /api/students?search=&classId=|section=&classGroup=&status=archived&page=&limit=` (Roster search: resolve pupil names / roll / admission no. / ); `GET /api/students/[id]` (Full pupil profile incl. sensitive fields); `GET /api/students/[id]/guardians[?review=1]` (Guardian relationships (relationshipId), current permissions); `GET /api/students/enrollments?studentId=` (Current/past enrollments (fromEnrollmentId), proposals (prop); `GET /api/students/parents?search=` (Find existing PARENT accounts (parentUserId) and their child); `GET /api/students/siblings?studentId=|search=` (Sibling group lookup (siblingStudentId / siblingGroupId)); `GET /api/students/next-roll?classId=` (Suggest the next free roll number for a class before create/); `GET /api/students/documents?studentId=` (List a pupil's documents (document id for delete)); `GET /api/students/timeline?studentId=` (Pupil timeline events); `GET /api/students/export` (CSV export of roster (bulk read, background admission)); `GET /api/admissions?view=active|mine|unassigned|overdue|all&status=&q= | ?forms=1 | ?id= | ?owners=1 | ?classes=1` (Application pipeline (application id, version), forms (formI); `GET /api/admission-queries?status=&source=&search=&overdue=true` (Enquiry list (enquiry id) for follow-up, conversion or start); `GET /api/admission-queries/follow-ups?queryId=` (Follow-up log for an enquiry); `GET /api/import-batches?kind=STUDENT_ROSTER|BANK_STATEMENT ; GET /api/import-batches/[batchId]` (Recent batches, staged rows and reviewToken); `GET /api/learning-actions?studentId= ; GET /api/learning-actions/[id]` (Learning actions (id, version) plus context: learners (stude); `GET /api/learning-transfers?studentId= ; GET /api/learning-transfers/[id]` (Transfer cases (caseId, version, mapping versions, consent r); `GET /api/portfolio?view=learners | ?view=evidence&studentId= | ?studentId= | ?view=shares | ?share=` (Taught learners, eligible submission versions (earlierVersio); `GET /api/pathways` (Overview: counsellor designations (counsellorId), staff list); `GET /api/accommodations?view=coordinator|staff|learner` (Access plans (planId, version) by class / for taught learner); `GET /api/care?view=me|students&q=|staff|clinic|safeguarding|settings` (Workspace capabilities, pupil search for confidential work, ); `GET /api/pickup/restrictions?q= | ?studentId=` (Find pupils for restrictions (studentId) and release history); `GET /api/pickup/gate` (Today's pickup checks (checkId) for gate staff); `GET /api/pickup?studentId=` (Guardian's own pickup authorizations (authorization id)); `GET /api/parent/data` (Guardian's linked children list (studentId) and permissions;); `GET /api/parent/invitations` (Pending guardian invitations (relationshipId) for the signed); `GET /api/parent/attendance?studentId=` (Child's absences/lates (attendanceId) to explain); `GET /api/family/activities?studentId=` (Activities and places (activityId, placeId, version)); `GET /api/family/meetings?studentId=` (Child's teachers, open windows/slots (windowId, startsAt), b); `GET /api/family/trips?studentId=` (Trip permission requests (tripId, version)); `GET /api/family/leave?studentId=` (Guardian's leave requests (id, status)); `GET /api/family/transfers` (Transfer cases awaiting guardian consent (caseId)); `GET /api/family/digests?studentId= ; GET /api/family/transport?studentId= ; GET /api/family/care-messages ; GET /api/parent/timetable|exam-datesheet|inbox|learning-actions` (Read-only family views (weekly digests, journey status, rele); `GET /api/student/today?day=` (Student's Today read model)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- None found for this cluster in src/app/actions/*.ts: the only 'use server' files are addCampus, settings, school-boards, locale, completeOnboarding, completeTeacherOnboarding, dashboard, marks, invite, auth/logout, auth/reset; none writes students, admissions, guardians or family records (dashboard.ts only reads roster/admission counts, e.g. dashboard.ts:118,585; marks.ts:15 saveMarks belongs to the exams/marks cluster).
- src/lib/students/enrollment.ts:applyWithdrawal (102) has no route in this cluster; it is reachable only through /api/academic/rollover, the typed op setup.apply_changes WITHDRAWALS (src/lib/school-ai/school-setup-service.ts:143) and src/lib/identity/service.ts.
- Pupil-roster UI (src/app/admin/page.tsx:451,753,763,818; src/components/shared-admin/index.tsx:2391-2430; src/components/GuardianAccessManager.tsx:144-175; src/app/pupils/[id]/page.tsx:71-100) calls the API routes listed in rows; no page performs a write without an API route.

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Placement invariant enforced in the DB: trigger pupil_identity_guard rejects any change to students.class_id/campus_id/roll_no unless app.enrollment_reviewed is set for that pupil (prisma/migrations/20261008081700_student_enrollment/migration.sql:111-117); applyEnrollment sets it (src/lib/students/enrollment.ts:73). Adapters must move pupils only via applyEnrollment/applyWithdrawal, never a direct student.update. A first StudentEnrollment row is created by trigger pupil_first_enrollment AFTER INSERT (migration.sql:126).
- Role predicates resolved: canManageCampusOps = SUPER_ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN (src/lib/api/scope.ts:230-232; APP_OWNER and PRINCIPAL excluded); canManageOperations/canManageAcademics = SUPER_ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN|PRINCIPAL (scope.ts:219-226); isCampusOperator = CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN (src/lib/auth/institution.ts:35); canConfigureCampus = ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN (src/lib/school-ai/setup-role.ts:2-3); isInstitutionAuthority = SUPER_ADMIN for GROUP, ADMIN for STANDALONE (institution.ts:24-27); canLeadAcademics = PRINCIPAL, or campus operator on own campus with no active principal (scope.ts:249-258).
- Permission matrix (V2 defaults, src/lib/permissions.ts:195-281; V1 rows written before 2026-10-26 resolve to V2 via isLegacyDefaultRow, permissions.ts:308-323): students add/edit/delete only APP_OWNER, SUPER_ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, ADMIN; every other staff role is students.view only. admissions: RECEPTIONIST view/add/edit (no delete); PRINCIPAL, TEACHER, ACCOUNTANT, LIBRARIAN have no admissions access. fees add: ACCOUNTANT yes, delete no. APP_OWNER/SUPER_ADMIN are fixed full-access (permissions.ts:285-287) but SUPER_ADMIN still fails role predicates like isCampusOperator/canConfigureCampus.
- Admissions separation of duties (src/lib/admissions/service.ts:40-49): intake (start, answers, staff-submit, request-info, notes, assign) is admissions.edit (front desk); decisions (offer, waitlist, reject, record-acceptance/decline), enrolment (convert) and the form designer require isCampusOperator. Receptionist and SUPER_ADMIN never decide. Owners must be CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN/RECEPTIONIST on the campus (service.ts:319-323). Enrolment requires ACCEPTED status and an explicit recorded duplicate decision when matches exist (service.ts:446-452).
- Capacity checks: assertPlanCapacity(metric 'students') guards both createStudentRecords (src/lib/students/create.ts:152,216) and convertApplication (src/lib/admissions/service.ts:470); class seat capacity for transfers is NOT computed server-side, only attested (capacityChecked flag, src/app/api/students/enrollments/route.ts:54; enrollment.ts:61; setup.apply_changes TRANSFERS carries verifiedCapacity, src/lib/school-ai/school-setup.ts:11).
- Guardian access is explicit and child-specific: new links start INVITED with all-false permissions (src/lib/students/create.ts:506-520); ACTIVE requires the guardian to accept with the invited email (src/app/api/parent/invitations/route.ts:28-41, guardians/route.ts:180). Family actions re-check guardianHasAccess per permission: communication (activities, meetings), attendance (leave, absence explanation), pickup (pickup), learningRecords (transfer consent, portfolio shares, pathways). An AI must never infer guardian links from names (enrollment.ts:60; automation ROLE_SETUP_INTERVIEW PARENT, src/lib/automation/role-setup.ts:13).
- Existing automation coverage in this cluster: tool students.create (ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN, own campus, students.add; catalog.ts:32, src/lib/automation/students.ts:13-26) which stages an ImportBatch with prisma.importBatch.upsert (students.ts:20) and then reuses createStudentRecords through a synthetic NextRequest (students.ts:21-22) with inviteAccounts:false; typed ops students.promote_enrollment and setup.apply_changes (GUARDIANS, TRANSFERS, WITHDRAWALS rows, school-setup.ts:10-12,22-24) are OPERATOR_TOOLS for CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN only (src/lib/school-ai/access.ts:56,70-75) and need ai.edit + students.view/edit (access.ts:351,358). No automation path exists for admissions, enquiries, family/parent, pickup, learning actions/transfers, portfolio, pathways, accommodations or care.
- Note the students.create adapter writes an ImportBatch row directly via prisma (src/lib/automation/students.ts:20) rather than going through /api/import-batches staging, so CSV-route per-row validation (roll/duplicate match keys, class resolution) is skipped; createStudentRecords still re-validates classes, roll uniqueness and plan capacity in its transaction (create.ts:216-234).
- Many handlers accept a preview/dry-run step that adapters should run first: DELETE /api/students?preview=true (route.ts:541), enrollments action 'preview' (enrollments/route.ts:73), consolidation 'preview' returning a token (consolidation/route.ts:44), import 'reversal-check'. Exception: guardians action 'preview' is NOT side-effect free; it can create a DRAFT relationship and always writes a guardianAccessEvent, and 'apply' must be by the same actor within 10 minutes (guardians/route.ts:133-170).
- Separation of duties present: care disclosure review must be a different LEAD/DEPUTY than the proposer (src/lib/care/service.ts:547); emergency access review by a LEAD other than the holder (care/service.ts:671). Missing where an adapter might assume it: enrollment propose->confirm by same registrar (enrollments/route.ts:53-66), pickup escalate->decide by same campus admin (src/lib/pickup/service.ts:212-217), learning-action activation by its own creator (src/lib/learning/actions.ts:125-143).
- Human-only or safety-critical actions that should not be AI-executed even when gates pass: pickup gate scan/release (physical identity attestation, src/lib/pickup/service.ts:161-172), pickup restriction lift, medication doses (dose must equal instruction, care/service.ts:321-322), safeguarding emergency access (care/service.ts:619-635), mentoring approval safeguarding/guardian-consent attestations (src/lib/pathways/service.ts:251-262), learning-transfer mapping review (mandatory teacher review, src/lib/learning/transfer.ts:181), trip consent (guardian's own decision).
- Confidential data that must stay out of model prompts: counselling notes (access-logged, never in exports, src/lib/pathways/service.ts:14-15,193-210), accommodation supporting records (audited on every read, src/lib/accommodations/service.ts:91-107), care/safeguarding records (confidentialAccessLog), pupil medical fields (medicalNotes/allergies/medications in PATCH /api/students), pickup codes (one-time bearer tokens returned by issueAuthorization/reissueCode). The engine already imports Pseudonymizer/scanForPII (src/lib/automation/service.ts:17).
- Scope gaps an adapter must not widen further: DELETE /api/admission-queries (route.ts:245) and POST /api/students/timeline (route.ts:55) only filter by school, not campus; pathways revokeCounsellor and verifyOpportunity update by id school-wide (src/lib/pathways/service.ts:89,164); admission-queries POST does not validate classInterestedId/assignedToId against the campus. Adapters should add the caller's campus filter before calling these.
- PATCH /api/students single-record edit can set status (incl. 'archived') without students.delete and without archivedAt or closing the enrollment (src/app/api/students/route.ts:360); classId/rollNo edits are refused with 409 (route.ts:323-328). Prefer setup.apply_changes WITHDRAWALS (applyWithdrawal, enrollment.ts:102-114) for leaving pupils and DELETE /api/students for administrative archive.
- Retired endpoints: POST /api/students/promote always 409 (promotion via /api/academic/rollover or students.promote_enrollment); GET/POST /api/parent/token answer 410 Gone. Do not map capabilities to them.
- Idempotency mechanisms to reuse: import batch fingerprint + review token (import-batches/route.ts ~231-253, src/lib/imports/review-token.ts:10); convertApplication replay (service.ts:441-444); portfolio idempotencyKey (portfolio/service.ts:77-82); trip consent idempotencyKey (trips.ts:182-191); meeting requestKey (meetings/service.ts:289-292); safeguarding concern idempotencyKey (care/service.ts:352-354); optimistic versions on admissions (body.version), learning actions (bump), transfers, accommodations (expectedVersion), care leadChange. Handlers without keys (enquiries, timeline notes, student documents, pickup restrictions, learning-action create, pathways creates) need an engine-side receipt like AUTOMATIC_ACTION_V1 (src/lib/automation/role-action-service.ts:22-54).
- Several routes emit notifications in the route layer rather than the service (family/activities route.ts:22-27, family/meetings route.ts:28-33, pickup/gate route.ts:22-36, care/route.ts:47-76, admissions assign inside actOnApplication service.ts:375-376). A service-level adapter must replicate the route's notification calls. Routes using assertSameOrigin (family/transfers, learning-transfers, pathways, accommodations) are CSRF guards only and are skipped safely when calling services directly.
- Cross-cluster adjacency (C3 services reached from routes outside this cluster): /api/public/admissions (unauthenticated family application flow: startApplication/saveApplication/submitFamilyApplication/familyRespond, src/lib/admissions/service.ts:140-245), /api/uploads/presign (student-docs keys, required before POST /api/students/documents), /api/attendance/leave (reviewLeave, staffLeaveQueue), /api/attendance/register (reviewExplanation), /api/trips (createTrip/reviseTrip/cancelTrip/tripRoster), /api/digests and /api/digests/[id] (prepare/edit/approve/schedule family digests), /api/school-ai/exams/[id]/accommodations (liveAdjustment), /api/academic/rollover (year-end promotion/withdrawal).

## C4 Teaching and assessment — 112 write capabilities

_Verifier: All 29 route.ts files under the 13 listed dirs were enumerated, and every POST/PATCH/DELETE action branch has an inventory row, so no in-scope write handler is missing. The orphan saveMarks claim in src/app/actions/marks.ts is confirmed, and no other 'use server' file writes in this domain. Errors found: 2 idempotency values are wrong (correction requests are "no"), 9 others marked "unknown" or unqualified are resolved above, 2 hidden report-card-generation side effects, GET /api/principal/queue is not read-only, and the billing note is wrong because requireAuthUser already asserts operational. Adjacent writers of marks and assignments under src/app/api/school-ai (lesson-plans publish, exams/[id]/gradebook, exams/[id]/repairs) are absent._

Without it, every call creates a new issue. (src/lib/certificates/service.ts:168 sets key = body.idempotencyKey ? String(...) : null. The replay lookup by campusId_idempotencyKey (176) runs only when the key is present.) |

| ⚑ `lesson_plans.publish_activity_as_assignment` | `POST /api/school-ai/lesson-plans (action=publish)` | SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, PRINCIPAL, TEACHER | CREATE | none (creates a DRAFT; release goes through assignments.release) | yes | none | direct | Outside the cluster's listed dirs, but it writes the assignments table through createAssignment and is not in the inventory or its adjacent-routes note. Replay returns {duplicate:true} from state.publishedActivities (lesson-plan-service.ts:140,143). That check runs in a separate transaction from createAssignment (129 vs 146), so two concurrent calls could both create a draft. Rubric is built from plan checks (150). |

It writes Mark rows (135) and moves the exam ACTIVE->MARKS_ENTRY (142) without going through /api/marks. A durable publication receipt makes replays return the same result (125-130). This is a second marks-writing path that the inventory's marks rows and adjacent-routes note both miss. |

On approve it updates official Mark rows (tx.mark.updateMany, 338), guarded by version + PENDING_REVIEW (244) and a fresh fingerprint (251-255, else STALE). This is a third marks-writing path the inventory does not mention. |
| ⚑ `school_ai_exams.resume_assessment_repair` | `POST /api/school-ai/exams/[id]/repairs (action=resume)` | SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, PRINCIPAL | UPDATE | requires a previously approved repair (status APPLYING/PARTIAL, 273) | yes | none | direct | Outside listed dirs. Each step commits once with its receipt (comment 284-286). Note: src/app/api/school-ai/exams/[id]/ has about 22 more subroutes (results-release, moderation, marking-proposal, attempts, ...) that this inventory does not cover. They may belong to a separate school-ai cluster. |

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (21):** `GET /api/exams?campusId=&classId=&status=&academicYear=&sessionId=` (Resolve exam titles/terms/classes to examId and current stat); `GET /api/marks?examId=` (Mark sheet: class roster (studentId, rollNo, name) and subje); `GET /api/assignments?classId=` (Author classes and assignments (ids, status, currentVersion)); `GET /api/assignments/[id]` (Assignment detail with submissions and submissionVersionIds ); `GET /api/resources?classId=&viewAs=&q=` (Search resources by title; classes the author can manage; la); `GET /api/marks-scan?examId=&subjectId=` (Templates and scan batches for an exam subject); `GET /api/marks-scan/[id]` (Batch review queue: cellIds, pages, version (knownVersion) f); `GET /api/marks-scan/template/[id]` (Printable sheet pages and QR codes); `GET /api/question-network` (Networks, items, review queue and shareable approved paper q); `GET /api/study` (Learner's approved study collection and recent question turn); `GET /api/study/teacher` (Published resources with passages, flagged turns (turnIds, l); `GET /api/planning?campusId=` (Baselines, scenarios (id, version), handoffs and eligible ow); `GET /api/activities[?id=]` (Activities with campus classes; roster gives placeIds, sessi); `GET /api/family/activities?studentId=` (Activities open to a child and the child's placeId/offer sta); `GET /api/improvement?view=workspace&frameworkId= | staff | sources | source&itemId= | pack&id=` (Frameworks/criteria ids, staff owners, linkable source recor); `GET /api/principal/queue?campusId=[&staff=1][&history=<key>]` (Queue item keys and assignable campus staff ids); `GET /api/corrections?kind=MARK|PAYMENT` (Correctable records (sourceId, version, original) and pendin); `GET /api/reports?examId=` (Report cards and review versions (reportCardId, versionId) f); `GET /api/reports/[id]/detail` (One report card detail); `GET /api/reports/generate-from-grades?studentId=&classId=&academicYear=` (Weighted final-grade preview before generating); `GET /api/front-desk/certificates?campusId= | issues=1&status=&q= | students=<query>` (Template ids, issued documents (id, version) and pupil searc)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- src/app/actions/marks.ts:saveMarks: a 'use server' action that writes prisma.mark.upsert behind a TEACHER-only gate plus subject ownership. Nothing imports it; the teacher marks page saves through fetch('/api/marks') (src/app/teacher/marks/page.tsx:137-151). This orphan action skips the DRAFT-status check, absent/exempt handling, the grade ladder, the ACTIVE->MARKS_ENTRY transition and notifications, so it must not be reused as an adapter.
- src/app/actions/dashboard.ts: cached read-only dashboard getters (getTeacherDashboardData:763, getPrincipalDashboardData:1023, getStudentDashboardData:1398, getCampusDashboardData:457). They write nothing.
- No other 'use server' file touches this cluster (checked src/app/actions/*.ts and grep over src). Every write in the cluster goes through the API routes listed.

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Coverage today: none of this cluster's write routes is reachable through src/lib/automation. catalog.ts:29-45 exposes only profile.update, platform.*, students.create, campus.create, library.create_books, front_desk.log_call, personal.save_plan, work.save, school.inspect, school.execute and curriculum.create. Of the 15 SCHOOL_AI_TOOLS (execution.ts:33-49), only exams.create_session_plan (office term exam session + exam rows, exam-session-plan-service.ts:95-106) and curriculum.import_topics touch assessment. Everything else needs new typed adapters.
- Academic-lead seat canLeadAcademics (src/lib/api/scope.ts:243-252): PRINCIPAL always; ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN only on their own pinned campus and only while it has no active principal (getCampusSeats); SUPER_ADMIN never. Used by exam review/publish (exams/route.ts:311), term-exam lock (lock/route.ts:35), report approve/review/publish (reports/route.ts:136), final grades (generate-from-grades/route.ts:80) and mark-correction approval (corrections/service.ts:51). An adapter must re-check it per campus at execution time.
- Inconsistent approval gates an adapter should not copy: PATCH /api/reports/[id] approve uses reviewer = SA/PRINCIPAL/ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN (reports/[id]/route.ts:34-37,64) rather than canLeadAcademics, and /api/exams/[id]/reject uses canReviewExam with no lead-seat check (reject/route.ts:23-25). For automation, apply the stricter canLeadAcademics.
- Exam ownership split (exam-permissions.ts:16-19,47-57): teachers create and move only QUIZ/CLASS_TEST for classes they teach; the office (PRINCIPAL/ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN) creates only MID_TERM/FINAL. SUPER_ADMIN cannot create or move exams (exams/route.ts:41-43) or lock them (lock/route.ts:16). CUSTOM is legacy and cannot be created.
- Exam lifecycle invariants: DRAFT -> ACTIVE -> MARKS_ENTRY -> LOCKED -> PRINCIPAL_REVIEWED -> PUBLISHED. Marks are accepted only when the exam is not DRAFT and not locked (marks/route.ts:84-89). Lock needs every student x subject mark (lock/route.ts:46-48) and generates report cards in the same transaction. Reject deletes those report cards and returns the exam to MARKS_ENTRY (reject/route.ts:101-118). PUBLISHED requires PRINCIPAL_REVIEWED (exams/route.ts:326-328). An exam with marks cannot return to DRAFT.
- Exam scheduling writes (withExamSchedulingWrite, exam-scheduling-write.ts:9-25) take the timetable campus lock, re-read the actor's role/campus/accessVersion, check exams module flags and call assertSchoolOperational. Adapters must go through this wrapper and not write prisma.exam directly.
- POST /api/marks has only a role predicate (marks/route.ts:13-15,39) and no exams permission-matrix check. Its campus check is skipped when user.campusId is null (marks/route.ts:81). Teachers are limited to their own subjects or classes where they are class teacher (94-110). An adapter should add assertPermission(exams, edit) and require a campus. Grades come from thresholdsForClass, and absent/exempt marks are stored as 0 with a null grade (146-160), so reuse this logic rather than reimplement it.
- Mark corrections are a role-based two-person flow: a TEACHER requests and the academic lead decides. Payment corrections: the accountant (or the campus admin when the campus has no accountant) requests and the campus admin decides (corrections/service.ts:30-52). Both need the reviewHash from preview to match (144,188) and the source version to be unchanged. An optional school policy, correctionSeparateApprover, applies (181-186). A PAYMENT decision needs a reason of at least 10 characters (171).
- Separation-of-duties rules in code: planning handoff proposer must differ from decider (planning/service.ts:173). Improvement evidence reviewer must differ from its creator and owner (improvement/service.ts:301). Question network: reviewer must not be the contributor (174), at least 2 unconflicted approvals are needed, and objections need adjudication by a leader who did not contribute (213-219). There is no SoD for marks-scan approve/commit (marks-scan/service.ts:190-249) or for certificate decide (certificates/service.ts:220). A leader issuing a certificate self-approves (certificates/service.ts:198).
- The question network is cross-tenant. Items and networks span schools, schools are invited by slug (question-network/service.ts:85-92), and adopters in other schools are notified through runWithTenantContext (192-203). Before sharing, the code checks for learner names (115-122) and refuses questions secured for upcoming or active exams (136-137). Answer keys must never reach learners. createNetwork/inviteSchool/acceptInvite skip the exams.view check that teaching() applies (40-43).
- Assignments (SKO-409) use no module permission matrix. Scope comes from canManageOperations: campus-wide for the office, school-wide for SA (assignments/service.ts:32-46). Teachers are limited by teacherClassIds. Release freezes an immutable version. Feedback stays private until returned. Gradebook transfer is only a REQUESTED flag and is never applied (service.ts:19-24). Student submit and paper receipt take idempotencyKey ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,120}$ (394). Learner submission actions are STUDENT-only.
- Actions that carry files cannot be done by a text-only adapter: assignment draft files (presigned PUT, assignments/service.ts:440-476), resource file versions (resources/service.ts:142-151) and marks-scan pages (base64 image + client-measured sharpness/contrast + QR, marks-scan/service.ts:102-111). Link-type resources can be automated.
- Activities: only the campus admin handles money and cancellation (waive, refund hand-off, cancel) (activities/service.ts:30-31,206,228,324). A refund goes to the finance adjustment workflow with idempotencyKey activity-refund-<placeId> (209). Capacity and waitlist are serialized by lockActivity. Guardian notifications are sent from the routes, not the service (activities/route.ts:24-29,36-41; family/activities/route.ts:22-27), so adapters must replicate them.
- Study assistant is STUDENT-only and spends AI credits (ensureAICreditsAvailable/consumeAICreditAndLog, study/assistant.ts:111-113). It is blocked during an active exam (101) and refuses PII (102). Answer keys and staff-only resources are excluded from passages (165). For office roles, addPassage/reviewFlag check school only, not campus (154-160,196-200).
- POST /api/reports/generate-from-grades creates a PUBLISHED FINAL exam and PUBLISHED report cards directly. It skips lock, review and version approval, and has no reports.edit permission or billing check (generate-from-grades/route.ts:71-176). Treat it as a high-risk PUBLISH that needs explicit confirmation.
- Report delivery is idempotent per report version (idempotencyBase report-version:<id>, notifications/service.ts:459) and requires the exam to be PUBLISHED (reports/route.ts:173-175; send/route.ts:47-49). Teachers may send single cards for their own classes (send/route.ts:12-14,34).
- Read-scope risk: GET /api/reports is open to any staff with reports.view, which defaults to true for ACCOUNTANT, LIBRARIAN and RECEPTIONIST in DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS (permissions.ts). It returns guardian WhatsApp and email (reports/route.ts:85-92) without any class restriction. AI lookups for those roles should not use it.
- Billing/operational gating is uneven. marks POST, exams POST/PATCH, exams reject and reports send call billingAccessResponse, and exam scheduling writes call assertSchoolOperational. The SKO-4xx services (assignments, resources, marks-scan, question-network, study, planning, activities, improvement, corrections, principal queue) do not. The automation engine imports assertSchoolOperational (automation/service.ts) and must keep calling it before every adapter.
- Principal queue: SUPER_ADMIN is read-only (queue.ts:41-44). act() filters keys through the caller's seats (collectSources 174-177). Keys outside the seat set come back as outcome 'resolved' with nothing written (281-286), and the adapter must report that as not applied. Snooze needs a reason of 5-300 characters and a time within 14 days.
- Planning writes only planning tables, never live records. PRINCIPAL can read but not write (planning/service.ts:12-19). Handoff approval re-checks the live snapshot fingerprint (176-178).
- Improvement frameworks are school-wide. Linking clinic sources requires active CLINIC confidential membership (improvement/service.ts:43-46). Completing an action needs evidence that another person reviewed and ACCEPTED, and that is CURRENT.
- Certificates (src/lib/certificates) are served from src/app/api/front-desk/certificates/route.ts, which may overlap with the front-desk cluster. Only never-approved template drafts can be deleted (certificates/service.ts:102-109).
- Adjacent routes outside this cluster's directories also write exam/mark/report tables and are not listed here: src/app/api/academic/exam-schedule, academic/exam-schedule/bulk, academic/exam-sessions, ai/generate-remarks, ai/review/[id]. Related assessment routes such as calculated-grades, grade-config, curriculum and learning-actions are also outside these directories.
- Many routes take Record<string, unknown> bodies with no zod schema: marks-scan, study, activities, principal queue, certificates, generate-from-grades. Adapters need their own strict zod schemas before calling the services.

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (28):** `/api/attendance?classId=&date=&campusId=` (Class roster with studentId, rollNo, name and today's marks ); `/api/attendance/register?classId=&date=` (Dated register: roster, current status (knownStatus), regist); `/api/attendance/register?queue=1&campusId=` (Exception queue: unmarked registers, unexplained absences, e); `/api/attendance/register?history=<attendanceId>` (One pupil-day's marks, corrections, explanations and version); `/api/attendance/register?rule=1` (Current published attendance counting rule); `/api/attendance/history?classId=` (Class attendance history); `/api/attendance/monthly?month=YYYY-MM&classId=|studentId=` (Monthly class/student attendance report using the published ); `/api/attendance/school-summary?period=today|week|month` (Campus attendance summary); `/api/attendance/leave?campusId=` (Open learner leave requests with ids, versions, student and ); `/api/meetings` (Teacher meeting desk: windows with slot states, bookings wit); `/api/communications?status=&channel=&studentId=&parentUserId=&scope=academic` (Family-communication delivery log (office) or academic deliv); `/api/communications/channel-preferences?campusId=` (Guardian ids, contacts and per-channel preference versions (); `/api/school-ai/announcements/options?campusId=` (Announcement classes/channels/templates for communications.q); `/api/notifications?view=all|mentions|actions&status=open|resolved` (Caller's inbox items with ids and counts); `/api/digests?classId=` (Class digests by week with ids, status, version, approvedVer); `/api/digests/[id]` (One digest with entry ids, statement ids, student names and ); `/api/whatsapp` (WhatsApp notification template keys); `/api/policies?view=manage` (All policy versions (ids, status) and escalated-question off); `/api/policies` (Caller's own policy questions (ids for feedback/escalate)); `/api/group-policy?view=campus&campusId=&domain=&at=` (Effective group/campus policy values, pending overrides, can); `/api/group-policy?view=library` (Templates, campuses, pending overrides, group-admin reviewer); `/api/group-policy?view=preview&versionId=&effectiveFrom=` (Impact preview and previewHash required for submit); `/api/group-policy?view=history&templateId= | view=provenance&recordType=&recordId=` (Template event history / record provenance); `/api/chat/directory?q=&role=` (People the caller may message (resolve names to userIds with); `/api/chat/conversations?filter=&q=` (Caller's conversations (ids, titles, unread)); `/api/chat/conversations/[id]` (Conversation detail with members and member roles); `/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages?cursor=&after=` (Message ids for edit/delete/reply); `/api/chat/settings | /api/chat/unread` (School messaging flags + canManage; total unread)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- None found: no server action under src/app/actions/*.ts writes attendance, meetings, communications, notifications, digests, WhatsApp, policies, group-policy or chat records.
- src/app/actions/dashboard.ts only reads attendance and communication summaries (imports at lines 16-17; attendance reads around lines 688 and 891; communicationSummary around lines 151 and 320). It is read-only.
- src/app/actions/invite.ts:125,176,508 calls notify(STAFF_INVITED / STAFF_REMOVED / INVITE_ACCEPTED) only as a side effect of staff invitation (another cluster), not as a user-facing notification feature.
- Background-only writers with no user route: dueDigests cron dispatch (src/lib/family/digests.ts:184), meeting reminders dueReminders/remind (src/lib/meetings/service.ts:374; notify.ts:37), the scheduled runNotificationAutomationSweep, and the group-policy activateDue/expireOverrides jobs (src/lib/group-policy/service.ts:81,103).

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Current AI coverage for C5 is only two typed operations: attendance.record_register and communications.queue_announcement (src/lib/school-ai/execution.ts:41,48). They are granted only through OPERATOR_TOOLS to CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN (src/lib/school-ai/access.ts:56), plus announcements for PRINCIPAL (access.ts:62). schoolAiOperationsFor returns an empty set for TEACHER and SUPER_ADMIN (access.ts:70-77). As a result, teachers, who mark most registers, cannot mark attendance through the automation catalog (src/lib/automation/catalog.ts:38-44).
- The AI attendance proposal allows only PRESENT/ABSENT/LEAVE and requires observationConfirmed:true (src/lib/school-ai/school-operations.ts:22). The real register supports PRESENT/LATE/PARTIAL/ABSENT/EXCUSED/LEAVE with arrival and departure times (src/lib/attendance.ts:23; src/lib/attendance/register.ts:103-108). The AI path refuses changes to a SUBMITTED register (school-operations-service.ts:115), needs attendance.edit to change existing marks (school-operations-service.ts:116), and rejects dates outside the editable academic cycle (school-operations-service.ts:104).
- Register writes are conflict-safe. Each entry must carry the knownStatus the person last saw. A mismatch becomes a conflict that is not written and returns HTTP 409 (register.ts:129; register/route.ts:56). An adapter must read GET /api/attendance/register first and must never set reviewedConflict:true without a human. Changing a submitted mark needs a reason of 5 to 500 characters plus attendance.edit, and changing another marker's mark in an open register also needs attendance.edit (register.ts:109,131-133). A register can be submitted only when no pupil is unmarked (register.ts:145-153). The future-date refusal exists only in the route (register/route.ts:52).
- Guardian side effects of attendance are partly in routes, not services. POST /api/attendance fires triggerRepeatedAbsenceAlert and notify ATTENDANCE_SUBMITTED in the route (src/app/api/attendance/route.ts:143-166), not in writeObservedAttendance. Absence alerts are deduped per student per day by idempotencyBase attendance-absence-3 (src/lib/notifications/automation.ts:93). saveRegister sends guardian correction notices itself (register.ts:162-176). writeObservedAttendance re-checks the actor's role, campus and accessVersion against the DB, plus assertSchoolOperational, under lockTimetableCampus (school-operations-register-write.ts:13-17).
- Permission matrix V2 defaults (src/lib/permissions.ts:195-279): CAMPUS_ADMIN, ADMIN, APP_OWNER and SUPER_ADMIN get the full matrix. PRINCIPAL has attendance view only (permissions.ts:205) and students view only. TEACHER uses the legacy V1 defaults: attendance view+add, no edit (permissions.ts:75,219). LIBRARIAN has no attendance access, and ACCOUNTANT/RECEPTIONIST have view only. Schools can override per role (moduleFlags, permissions.ts:316-323), so adapters must call assertPermission/assertWorkspacePermission live and never trust the static lists above.
- Learner leave: approving records the decision only and never marks the register (src/lib/family/leave.ts:76). TEACHER is explicitly excluded and attendance.edit is required (leave.ts:78-79). Decline and clarify require a note. There is an optimistic knownVersion check (leave.ts:89) under a FOR UPDATE lock. Explanation review: rejecting or asking to clarify requires a note, accepting writes an EXPLANATION-sourced correction and notifies guardians (register.ts:201-226).
- Meetings: every staff mutation is bound to the caller's own windows and bookings (teacherId = user.userId; src/lib/meetings/service.ts:67,94,163-169). Reschedule and cancel require knownVersion (service.ts:329,345). Slot races are settled by a partial unique index and return 409 (service.ts:300-305,334-336). The family sees a follow-up only once it is shared, with at most 5 actions, each with an owner role and a due date (service.ts:184-218). Staff notes are never readable by families. Notifications to the other party are sent in the route (src/app/api/meetings/route.ts:27-32), so adapters must call notifyParty/notifyConflicts/notifyFollowUp. Guardian booking is at /api/family/meetings, outside this cluster.
- Digests: drafts contain only evidence already released to families. Approval binds the exact version (approvedVersion === version), and any edit resets approval (src/lib/family/digests.ts:118,130). There is no separation of duties, so the preparer may approve and send (digests.ts:122-132). An AI adapter must stop at draft and ask a human to approve. Dispatch rechecks every source and each guardian's learningRecords access, blocks children whose source changed, and is unique per child/week/guardian/channel (digests.ts:155-181).
- WhatsApp manual send has no permission-matrix check, only a role list plus the whatsappEnabled plan gate (src/app/api/whatsapp/route.ts:32-34,53). approvedSchoolData is asserted by the caller and lifts the template's requiresApprovedData block (src/lib/notifications/service.ts:295), so an AI must never set it true without human-reviewed data. Sends are deduped only when relatedId is given (route.ts:111). Recipients are re-authorized per guardian for the communication, learningRecords or finances permission (service.ts:426).
- communications.queue_announcement requires assertApprovalRole, an accessVersion match, ai.edit, students.view+edit and an operational school (src/lib/school-ai/announcements-service.ts:16-25). PRINCIPAL is offered the tool but lacks students.edit by default, so execution fails. The WhatsApp channel needs an exact approved template for every language; SMS is capped at 1600 characters (src/lib/school-ai/announcements.ts:16-17).
- Guardian channel consent: enabling a channel needs the current matching guardian contact, verified=true, consent=true and evidence of at least 10 characters (announcement-preferences-service.ts:35; announcement-preferences.ts:3). A PARENT may only opt itself out (service.ts:28). There is an optimistic expectedVersion check plus an advisory lock (service.ts:31-33). An AI must never infer consent. An enabled WhatsApp preference is what lets the inbound bot identify a guardian (src/lib/whatsapp/inbound-guardian.ts:18-20).
- The automation engine auto-approves its own workspace record because the user explicitly asked for the action, while domain approvals stay in the services (src/lib/automation/service.ts:79-81). Domain approvals that new adapters must not self-satisfy are: digest approve, policy approve, group-policy review and override decisions, and explanation and leave decisions.
- Group policy separation of duties: a requester can never decide their own override (src/lib/group-policy/service.ts:229). Only the named reviewer can review a version (service.ts:376). Self-review is allowed only while the author is the sole active group admin and gives a reason of at least 10 characters; it is audited and a notice is fired (service.ts:379-399). Submit binds a previewHash and returns 409 if campus state changed (service.ts:363). Everything returns 404 for STANDALONE schools (service.ts:53). Overrides last at most 400 days, and requests on locked keys are denied with a LOCK_DENIED event (service.ts:206-216).
- Policies: only SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN and CAMPUS_ADMIN manage them, and the principal only reads (src/lib/policies/service.ts:16-17,41). Approval has no separation of duties (service.ts:83-97). Approved versions are immutable and change only through a new version (service.ts:66,74-81). Escalation requires confirmedOffice === office, meaning the asker reviewed the recipient (service.ts:138). PolicyInput.campusId is not validated against the actor's campus (service.ts:61-72).
- Chat: APP_OWNER is excluded because of the unscoped tenant context (src/lib/chat/service.ts:69-75). DIRECT threads are re-authorized on every send (service.ts:617-626). Families cannot mark messages urgent (service.ts:639). Sending is rate-limited to 40 per minute (messages/route.ts:54). Groups are staff-only and ANNOUNCEMENT channels leadership-only (src/lib/chat/policy.ts:309-316). Conversations have at most 500 members. addMembers requires an OWNER or MODERATOR member role even for leadership (service.ts:1005). Leadership may soft-delete any message (policy.ts:331-333; service.ts:921-927). School messaging settings are writable only by the institution authority (policy.ts:323-325).
- Notifications: marking items read is attention only and never resolves ACTION items (src/app/api/notifications/route.ts:46). Opening an item rechecks access to its source (src/app/api/notifications/[id]/open/route.ts:18-19). These are inline Prisma writes filtered only by userId (route.ts:49-60).
- Reminder sweep (POST /api/communications): a SUPER_ADMIN campusId is passed through unvalidated, and omitting it runs school-wide (route.ts:108). The sweep fires fee-due, fee-overdue, marks-deadline and principal-review reminders (src/lib/notifications/automation.ts:381-405), each deduped by an idempotency key.
- The WhatsApp webhook (POST /api/whatsapp/webhook) is a system endpoint authenticated by Meta HMAC (route.ts:29). It processes each msg.id once (inbound-guardian.ts:40-43) and pseudonymizes before calling the AI (inline-guardian.ts:46). It must never be exposed as an AI capability.
- Family-side writers for this domain live outside the cluster directories and are needed for PARENT and STUDENT automation: /api/family/leave (POST requestLeave, PATCH updateOwnLeave), /api/family/meetings (POST bookMeeting and family reschedule/cancel/complete), /api/parent/attendance (POST submitExplanation, PARENT only, register.ts:178-199) and /api/family/digests (GET). Announcement deliveries are read via /api/school-ai/workspace/[id]/announcements.
- src/lib/email (smtp.ts, readiness.ts, templates) has no API route in this cluster. Outbound email reaches families only through the notification and announcement services.

## C6 Finance, payroll, purchasing and inventory — 120 write capabilities

| key | route | roles | risk | approval | idem. | AI today | reuse | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fees.collect_payment` | `POST /api/fees/collect` | SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, ACCOUNTANT | FINANCIAL | none | yes | partial | wrapper | Body {studentId, invoiceId, amount minor, fineAmount?, discountAmount?, paymentDate, paymentMethod CASH\|BANK\|CHEQUE\|SAFEPAY\|MOBILE_WALLET, referenceNumber?, note?, idempotencyKey required} (collect/route.ts:33-62).
