**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (44):** `/api/fees/invoices` (Search invoices by campusId/status/classId/search/month with); `/api/fees/invoices/[invoiceId]` (Invoice detail incl. balance/status before payment, cancel o); `/api/fees/payments` (List payments/receipts to resolve paymentId for refunds and ); `/api/fees/student/[studentId]` (A student's fee schedule/invoices (staff or family)); `/api/fees/resolve` (Resolve a student's applicable fee lines for an academic yea); `/api/fees/types` (Fee types by campus (name/code -> feeTypeId)); `/api/fees/groups` (Fee groups by campus (name -> feeGroupId); GATE GAP: require); `/api/fees/master-lines` (Lines in a fee group (feeGroupId filter); GATE GAP: requireA); `/api/fees/assignments` (Class to fee-group assignments per year; GATE GAP: requireAu); `/api/fees/discounts` (Discounts by campus (name/code -> discountId); GATE GAP); `/api/fees/discount-assignments` (Discounts assigned to students (studentId/discountId filters); `/api/fees/fine-rules` (Fine rules by campus; GATE GAP); `/api/fees/carry-forwards` (Carry-forward balances by studentId/academicYear; GATE GAP, ); `/api/fees/structure` (Legacy class fee structures (campusId/classId); GATE GAP); `/api/fees/collect` (Payment window for an invoice (payments list); GATE GAP, sch); `/api/fees/adjustments` (Adjustments for campus/invoice with canDecide/needsReason fl); `/api/fees/payment-plans` (Instalment plans for an invoice (with version for PATCH)); `/api/fees/reconciliations` (Pending/posted bank reconciliations for a campus); `/api/fees/reports/defaulters` (Students with overdue balances (targets for reminders)); `/api/fees/reports/collection` (Collection totals for a period); `/api/fees/campus/summary` (Campus fee summary); `/api/fees/statement` (Student statement for an academic year); `/api/fees/family-statement` (Family statement across children (studentScope finances)); `/api/fees/generation-job/[jobId]` (Status of an invoice generation job); `/api/fees/pay-online` (Online payment availability for an invoice or status of an o); `/api/payroll` (Legacy payroll run + lines + payment methods for campus/mont); `/api/payroll/runs` (Policies, providers, calculation versions, submissions, empl); `/api/payroll/report` (Payroll report for a date range); `/api/accounts/chart` (Chart of accounts (name -> accountId, type)); `/api/accounts/ledger` (Ledger rows + totals by currency/kind/account/source/status/); `/api/accounts/bank-accounts` (Bank accounts (name -> bankAccountId, currency)); `/api/accounts/payment-methods` (Payment method refs (name -> paymentMethodId)); `/api/accounts/profit` (Income/expense profit summary for a period); `/api/tax?view=overview|documents|document` (Tax package catalog, activations (draft ids), documents); `/api/billing` (SaaS subscription snapshot/plans); `/api/billing/lifecycle` (Subscription state and recent subscription requests (non-own); `/api/campus-packages?view=states|canteen|boarding|family` (Package status, operators, menu items, orders, boarders, lea); `/api/purchasing` (Budgets with committed/available, requests (ids, versions), ); `/api/inventory/items` (Items with stock per store; search by name); `/api/inventory/stores` (Stores (name -> storeId)); `/api/inventory/suppliers` (Suppliers (name -> supplierId)); `/api/inventory/categories` (Item categories); `/api/inventory/transactions` (Stock movements by kind/store); `automation tool school.inspect (op finance.apply_operation / fees.generate_invoices)` (Named options (invoices with balance, payments, students, ac)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:previewLocaleChange / applyLocaleChange: schedule a school or campus locale change, including currency (a currency change goes to FINANCE_REVIEW, locale.ts:126). School scope requires the institution authority (canManageSchool, src/lib/locale/package.ts:101); a campus operator may change delegated fields only. No API route.
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:reviewLocaleCurrency (line 140): an ACCOUNTANT approves or rejects a pending currency change made by someone else (createdBy != reviewer). No API route.
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:setLocaleDelegation (line 131): the institution authority delegates locale fields (including currency) to a campus. No API route.
- src/app/actions/settings.ts:previewCampusArchive / archiveCampus (lines 413, 420): archive refused while a campus has open invoices or pending online payments (finance blockers). Institution owner only.
- src/app/actions/dashboard.ts: read-only finance summaries (invoice totals by status, collections, plan). No writes.
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:saveOnboardingStep / finishOnboarding: the setup wizard writes no fee, payroll or accounts records; fees and payroll are listed as deferred optional work (src/lib/onboarding/setup-state.ts:252-254).

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Today's AI coverage for this cluster is only two typed operations: finance.apply_operation (PAYMENT, REFUND, CREDIT, OPENING_BALANCE, EXPENSE, RECONCILE, SCENARIO) and fees.generate_invoices (src/lib/school-ai/execution.ts:39,43; src/lib/school-ai/finance-operations.ts:11-19; src/lib/school-ai/fee-invoices.ts:8-13). Nothing in src/lib/automation/catalog.ts covers payroll, accounts setup, tax, purchasing, inventory, campus packages or subscriptions.
- Who can run the two finance operations: ACCOUNTANT always; CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN only while their campus has no accountant seat (financeDesk); SUPER_ADMIN and PRINCIPAL never (src/lib/school-ai/access.ts:56-58,70-76,84-89). Execution needs an approved workspace version, previewHash and idempotencyKey (execution.ts:49-55), plus re-checked ai view+edit, fees view+add+edit, accounts view+add and an operational school (fee-invoices-service.ts:16-26; finance-operations-service.ts:18-24).
- Approval separation on the AI path is weak: assertApprovalRole lets an ACCOUNTANT approve their own finance.apply_operation and fees.generate_invoices versions (access.ts:155-158). Only REFUND enforces correctionSeparateApprover (finance-operations-service.ts:94-98; schema default true at prisma/schema.prisma:51).
- Governance bypass to fix before expanding: AI CREDIT edits invoice totals directly (finance-operations-service.ts:143-145), and AI REFUND posts a reversal payment at once (:149-155). The route path makes the finance desk request, the campus admin decide, and records disbursement as a separate step (src/lib/fees/adjustments-service.ts:10-18,96-114,153-161). Adapters for credits, waivers and refunds should call requestAdjustment and leave the decision to a human.
- Payment method enums do not match: /api/fees/collect takes CASH|BANK|CHEQUE|SAFEPAY|MOBILE_WALLET (collect/route.ts:39-41); /api/fees/payment takes lowercase cash|bank_transfer|card|mobile_wallet|cheque (src/lib/validators/schemas.ts:398); AI PAYMENT takes CASH|BANK_TRANSFER|CHEQUE|CARD|OTHER (finance-operations.ts:12). An adapter must normalize these.
- Payment invariants in recordPayment: the invoice row is locked FOR UPDATE, receipt numbers come from a per-campus/year counter, and overpayment becomes carry-forward credit (src/lib/fees/payment.ts:32-58). /api/fees/collect requires idempotencyKey + requestHash (collect/route.ts:56-69); /api/fees/payment has no key (payment/route.ts:44-49). Base adapters on collect.
- Invoice generation: the route locks the campus, re-checks access inside the transaction (src/lib/fees/invoice-generation.ts:25-46) and refuses a month that already has invoices (generate-invoices/route.ts:63-68). It applies package tax through taxFor/issueDocument (route:6,242). AI fees.generate_invoices refuses any campus with an approved tax package (fee-invoices-service.ts:113).
- Default role matrix (V2, permissions.ts:195-281, cut-over at :283, applied by moduleFlags :316-323). ACCOUNTANT: fees view/add/edit (no delete), accounts view/add/edit (no delete), payroll view/add, inventory none. PRINCIPAL, TEACHER, LIBRARIAN, RECEPTIONIST: no fees, accounts, payroll or inventory. Families: fees view only. APP_OWNER and SUPER_ADMIN are fixed full-matrix roles (permissions.ts:285-287), so matrix-only gates (ledger, bank-accounts, collect, payment-plans, reconciliations) also pass for APP_OWNER inside its own school and for any role a school custom-grants. Adapters should add canManageFinance (scope.ts:235).
- Campus fence gaps: many PATCH/DELETE handlers call scopedCampusWhere(user) with no campusId, which means school-wide (master-lines/route.ts:78,113; types/route.ts:65,101; fine-rules/route.ts:68,106; discounts/route.ts:84,128; groups/route.ts:70,99; assignments/route.ts:75). Carry-forward POST looks students up school-wide (carry-forwards/route.ts:55-58). Discount assignment never checks that discount and student share a campus (discount-assignments/route.ts:39-47). Fee structure PUT/DELETE are school-wide (structure/[structureId]/route.ts:24-26,96-98), and POST does not check classId against the campus (structure/route.ts:66-69). Payment-method PATCH/DELETE and bank-account DELETE are school-wide too. Adapters must pin writes to user.campusId themselves.
- Read gaps: GET fees groups, master-lines, assignments, discounts, discount-assignments, fine-rules, carry-forwards, structure and the collect GET check only requireAuthUser, so families and teachers can read balances and student names school-wide (e.g. carry-forwards/route.ts:10-30; discount-assignments/route.ts:8-31; collect/route.ts:135-150). AI lookups must run assertFeesRead (scope.ts:166) first.
- Maker-checker rules an adapter must preserve: (1) adjustments are decided by canApproveFinance, with SD3 sole-checker self-decision only with a reason of 10+ characters (scope.ts:240-243,294-314). (2) A payment plan's approver must differ from its proposer (payment-plans/route.ts:78), but no role seat is required. (3) A payroll approver differs from the preparer when policy.requireSeparation is set (payroll-runs/service.ts:259; default true at schema.prisma:8593), with a reason of 10+ characters. (4) Purchasing: maker differs from checker unless SD3, and the approval limit and budget are enforced (purchasing/service.ts:128-139). (5) A tax package is approved by the institution authority with SD3 (tax/service.ts:156-166). (6) A currency change enters FINANCE_REVIEW and must be reviewed by an ACCOUNTANT other than its creator (src/app/actions/locale.ts:126,140-157).
- There are two payroll engines. The legacy one (/api/payroll/generate + /api/payroll/lines) is gated by canManageCampusOps, so ACCOUNTANT is excluded despite holding payroll.add. It marks lines PAID and posts EXPENSE ledger entries with no approval step, and it only collects TEACHER/PRINCIPAL/CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN/SUPER_ADMIN (generate/route.ts:89-105). The SKO-434 engine (/api/payroll/runs) has the finance desk prepare and the campus admin approve, covers 8 staff roles (payroll-runs/service.ts:25-27), and treats export as separate from payment. Adapters should target the runs service.
- Ledger invariants: a manual entry's account type must equal its kind and the account must be active, and closed bank periods are refused (ledger-service.ts:116-125; ledger-protection.ts:34). Fee and payroll postings cannot be reversed or deleted from the ledger (ledger-service.ts:147-149; ledger-protection.ts:20-21). System accounts stay active with their type (chart/route.ts:97-99).
- The SaaS subscription is separate from tuition. canManageSubscription means institution authority plus the owner or purchase-delegate flag (src/lib/auth/institution.ts:24-31). Cancel, refund and the billing portal require mfaVerified (billing/lifecycle/route.ts:56-58; stripe/portal/route.ts:14). Checkout requires a platform-reviewed plan change (stripe/checkout/route.ts:52-53). Requests are idempotent through saveSubscriptionRequest (src/lib/billing/lifecycle.ts:61-73). Keep these out of automatic execution, or require explicit human confirmation.
- Webhooks are authenticated by provider signature and run unscoped (safepay/notification/route.ts:26-46; stripe/webhook/route.ts:181-200). /api/safepay/simulate has no auth outside production (simulate/route.ts:12-31). None of these may be exposed as automation tools.
- Purchasing and inventory never consult the permission matrix (no assertPermission in src/app/api/inventory/* or src/lib/purchasing/service.ts). Inventory writes write no audit log. Purchasing submit, receive, return, invoice and close look requests up school-wide (purchasing/service.ts:99,166,210,232,274).
- Campus package operators are per-user designations stored on the package, not roles (campus-packages/service.ts:52-56), so the setup action is effectively an access grant. Canteen ordering and spend limits are PARENT-only with guardian finances permission (:158-160). Dietary and boarding data are sensitive child records.
- Onboarding questionnaire: setup does not create finance records. FULL_OPERATIONS defers 'feesPayroll' as optional post-launch work (src/lib/onboarding/setup-state.ts:242-255), and ACCOUNTANT's launch journey is finance/fees (src/lib/onboarding/launch-plan.ts:86). Currency comes from the locale package and is snapshotted into fee groups, discounts and bank accounts (groups/route.ts:45; discounts/route.ts:62-63). A safe auto-setup order is: locale/currency, then chart of accounts, bank accounts and payment methods, then fee types, fee groups, master lines, class assignments, discounts and fine rules, then an optional tax package draft (needs authority sign-off), then invoices. Payroll needs a policy and a provider mapping before any run.
- Related flows outside this cluster: bank-statement staging lives in /api/import-batches/[batchId] and src/lib/jobs/bank-import.ts (/api/fees/bank-import returns 410). Payment corrections live in /api/corrections (src/lib/corrections/service.ts). Platform review of subscription requests lives in /api/owner/subscription-requests.
- Campus archive is blocked by open invoices and pending online payments (src/app/actions/settings.ts:385-395). Reminders go only to guardians with verified WhatsApp consent and financial access, and report provider acceptance, not delivery (fees/remind/route.ts:20-23).

## C7 Campus operations — 112 write capabilities

_Verifier: I checked all 27 route.ts files under the 10 dirs. Every exported POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE handler and action branch is in the inventory. The gaps: it missed two writes inside GET handlers (certificate PDF saved on first download; library holds expired during lookup and overview), and 10 spot-checks found field errors (complaint-assign idempotency, return-copy risk, vehicle/route create idempotency, certificate read roles, and notes on issue notifications, CSV export, legacy issue and stop events). No server action or 'use server' file writes these domains._

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (22):** `GET /api/front-desk/visitors?campusId=&status=ON_SITE|LEFT|REFUSED|all&q=&date=` (Find a visit id by visitor name/host/reference (for check-ou); `GET /api/front-desk/postal?owners=1 | ?history=<id> | ?view=active|mine|closed|all&direction=&q=` (Resolve postal item ids and front-office owner ids (CAMPUS_A); `GET /api/front-desk/complaints?owners=1 | ?id= | ?status=&owner=me|unassigned&overdue=1&q=` (Resolve complaint ids (queue shows minimal metadata, no narr); `GET /api/front-desk/phone-calls?owners=1 | ?history=<callbackId> | ?view=open|mine|overdue|unassigned|all&direction=&q=` (Resolve call/callback ids by caller name, purpose or phone d); `GET /api/front-desk/certificates?campusId= | ?issues=1&status=&q= | ?students=<query>` (Resolve template ids, issued document ids and pupil ids (nam); `GET /api/library/desk?code=<memberNo|barcode> | ?preview=<barcode>&on= | (none: overview)` (Resolve a member card or copy barcode to ids, open loans, ho); `GET /api/library/books?campusId=&search=` (Find book ids by title/author/ISBN); `GET /api/library/categories?campusId=` (Resolve category ids by name); `GET /api/library/members?campusId=` (Resolve library member ids/memberNo by user full name); `GET /api/library/issues?campusId=&status=issued|returned` (Resolve loan (issue) ids for renew/lost/return); `GET /api/transport/routes?campusId=` (Resolve route ids by title with vehicles and rider counts); `GET /api/transport/vehicles?campusId=` (Resolve vehicle ids by number); `GET /api/transport/riders?routeId= | ?q=` (Route detail with stops and vehicles; find student ids by na); `GET /api/transport/trips?date= | ?id= | ?id=&receiversFor=<studentId> | ?view=staff` (Resolve trip ids for a day, trip manifest, eligible receiver); `GET /api/trips?campusId= | ?id=` (List trip notices; roster with each guardian answer); `GET /api/dormitory/allocations?lookup=students&q= | ?campusId=&date=&studentId=&roomId=` (Find student ids for allocation; occupancy per room and stay); `GET /api/dormitory/rooms?campusId=` (Resolve room ids by number with today's occupancy); `GET /api/dormitory/room-types?campusId=` (Resolve room type ids by name); `GET /api/service-desk?view=mine | queue&campusId=&status= | form&campusId=&text=` (Own requests, campus queue, request categories with keyword-); `GET /api/service-desk/[id] (?attachment=<eventId>)` (Request detail with knownVersion needed for every action); `GET /api/data-lifecycle (| ?preview=<category>)` (Schedules, holds, requests (with versions) and, for PARENT, ); `GET /api/integrations?view=connections | deliveries&connectionId=&status= | delivery&id=` (Resolve connection and delivery ids (no secrets returned))

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- None found for this cluster. The only server-action code that touches these domains is read-only dashboard aggregation: src/app/actions/dashboard.ts:librarianSummary (:1627, counts libraryMember) and src/app/actions/dashboard.ts:receptionistSummary (:1764, counts visitors, complaints, calls, callbacks, postal and certificate templates). No other src/app/actions/*.ts file or 'use server' file writes front-desk, library, transport, trip, dormitory, service-desk, data-lifecycle or integration models. The UI components (src/components/operations/*, src/components/library/lending-desk.tsx, src/components/transport/transport.tsx, src/components/service-desk/service-desk.tsx, src/components/integrations/integrations.tsx, src/components/settings/DataLifecyclePanel.tsx, src/components/family/staff-requests.tsx) call the API routes listed in rows.

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Role predicates resolved: canManageFrontDesk = SUPER_ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN|PRINCIPAL|RECEPTIONIST (src/lib/api/scope.ts:264 via canManageOperations/canManageAcademics :219-226); canManageLibrary = SUPER_ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN|PRINCIPAL|LIBRARIAN (scope.ts:260); canManageCampusOps = SUPER_ADMIN|CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN (scope.ts:230; isCampusOperator = CAMPUS_ADMIN|ADMIN, src/lib/auth/institution.ts:35); isInstitutionAuthority = SUPER_ADMIN in GROUP institutions, ADMIN in STANDALONE (institution.ts:24-27, fails closed when kind is unknown).
- Permission matrix: APP_OWNER/SUPER_ADMIN always pass (src/lib/permissions.ts:285-287,346). V2 defaults deny PRINCIPAL library/front-desk/transport/dormitory (permissions.ts:213-217); LIBRARIAN holds library only (:254); RECEPTIONIST front-desk only (:274). So routes that check the matrix exclude PRINCIPAL by default, while routes that only check a predicate do not.
- Inconsistent gates an adapter should NOT copy blindly: library books/categories/members/issues and the lending desk check canManageLibrary but never the matrix (books/route.ts:49,83,128; desk.ts:29), so PRINCIPAL can write the library. Transport riders/trips and /api/trips use canManageOperations (includes PRINCIPAL, no matrix) (transport/service.ts:33; family/trips.ts:43) while transport routes/vehicles use canManageCampusOps + matrix, and scope.ts:228 says PRINCIPAL never holds campus ops (C31). Dormitory rooms/room-types skip the matrix (rooms/route.ts:44; room-types/route.ts:33). The existing automation adapters already combine predicate + assertWorkspacePermission (role-action-service.ts:17-18,41-42) - follow that pattern.
- Campus scoping gaps in legacy library routes: PATCH/DELETE on books, categories, members and the legacy issues routes look rows up by campus.schoolId only, not the caller's campus (books/route.ts:89-91,134-136; categories/route.ts:63-65,95-97; members/route.ts:72-74; issues/route.ts:50-58,95-97). Desk renew/lost/charge look up by schoolId only (desk.ts:169,216,231). An adapter must add own-campus checks.
- Tenant guard: creates without schoolId (books, categories, members, vehicles, routes, room types, rooms, legacy issues) rely on the fail-closed Prisma guard stamping schoolId and validating references (src/lib/db/prisma.ts:142,201). Most new services also run in schoolAiTransaction (serializable, sets app.current_school_id, src/lib/school-ai/transaction.ts:13-17).
- Existing automation coverage in this cluster is only two tools: library.create_books (catalog.ts:34; role-action-service.ts:40-47) and front_desk.log_call (catalog.ts:35; role-action-service.ts:16-21). None of the 15 school.execute operations in src/lib/school-ai/execution.ts touches front desk, library, transport, trips, dormitory, service desk, data lifecycle, integrations or uploads. school.inspect (assistant-catalog) has no lookups for these domains, so the AI cannot resolve names to ids here today.
- The automation entry requires ai permission (src/lib/automation/service.ts:33); V2 gives LIBRARIAN and RECEPTIONIST ai view+add (permissions.ts V2 rows), so they can reach their tools.
- Idempotency: visitors, postal, complaints, phone calls, certificate issue and dorm allocations accept an optional idempotencyKey matching /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{8,80}$/ with a requestHash; a mismatched replay returns 409 (visitors.ts:63-74; postal.ts:72-80; complaints.ts:66-74; phone-calls.ts:79-104; certificates/service.ts:168-178; allocations.ts:100-110). Service desk requires a key (service.ts:117-118). Transport crew events dedupe on clientEventId (transport/service.ts:303). /api/trips POST, library desk, legacy library, vehicles/routes creates, integrations and legal holds have no key: wrap them in the automation receipt (schoolAiRequest AUTOMATIC_ACTION_V1) as role-action-service.ts:25-27 does.
- Optimistic concurrency: front-desk PATCH actions accept an optional version (visitors.ts:121; postal.ts:111; complaints.ts:94; phone-calls.ts:130; certificates decide :228). Service desk requires knownVersion (service-desk/service.ts:209). /api/trips revise requires knownVersion (family/trips.ts:73). Data lifecycle schedule/review require expectedVersion/version (data-lifecycle/service.ts:92,195). Adapters should always read then pass the version.
- Capacity and seat checks to preserve: transport seats = largest active vehicle; rider assignment refused when full (transport/service.ts:49-52,141-145), route vehicles must seat current riders (:103-104), vehicle shrink/deactivate guarded (:112-124), trip overflow opens a CAPACITY_OVERFLOW exception (:200,281). Dormitory peak occupancy per room over the date range, out-of-service rooms, student double-booking (allocations.ts:73-82); room capacity/status changes checked against current and future stays with dryRun (rooms/route.ts:122-131). Library maxLoans, renewal limit, reservation queue and hold expiry (desk.ts:105,108-111,172-173,192-210).
- Approvals and separation of duties: certificate issue goes to PENDING_APPROVAL for receptionists when the template requires approval (always for ID cards) and only CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN decide (certificates/service.ts:74,198,221) - campus operators self-approve their own issues and templates (no maker!=checker check). Library charge WAIVE is CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN only, with a reason (desk.ts:19,229). Confidential complaints are assigned only by leaders (complaints.ts:65,98). Data lifecycle review must be by a different institution authority than the requester (data-lifecycle/service.ts:194). In a STANDALONE school the ADMIN is the only authority and ADMIN is not invitable (institution.ts:23 comment), so retention-deletion and migration requests may have no possible reviewer (whether a second authority can exist: unknown). Service desk closing is the requester's confirmation (service-desk/service.ts:294).
- Human attestations that AI must never assert: transport vehicle check signature must equal the actor's full name (transport/service.ts:444), handover identityChecked (:399), crew boarding/absence/stop events are field observations; postal delivery evidence (postal.ts:148). Visitor records never authorize collecting a child (visitors.ts:10).
- Notifications fire from both services and routes: postal and phone-call owners are notified inside the service (postal.ts:53-56,99; phone-calls.ts:72-75,117,180), but transport exceptions (transport/trips/route.ts:33,38,47,58,67) and service-desk events (service-desk/route.ts:32-36; [id]/route.ts:27) are sent by the route after the service returns. An adapter that calls the service directly must also send those notifications.
- PII and confidentiality: confidential complaint narratives are hidden from non-leaders (complaints/route.ts:87-89; complaints.ts:23-28); sensitive call notes visible only to leaders, recorder and owners (phone-calls.ts:65-67); visitor phones masked for non-front-desk readers and redacted after 180 days (visitors/route.ts:23,43; visitors.ts:13,40-43); certificate previews and data-lifecycle exports carry full learner PII. Per the AI PII policy these must be pseudonymized or kept out of prompts.
- Secrets: integrations create, rotate-key, rotate-signing and webhook return one-time API keys or signing secrets (integrations/service.ts:72-73,86,93,128); data-lifecycle exports return a download token. These must never enter model context, conversation logs or automation receipts. Integration test, replay, deliver-now and webhook changes cause outbound HTTP to customer URLs, which breaks the engine rule against generic HTTP. Treat the whole integrations domain as not AI-automatable.
- Uploads (/api/uploads/presign, /api/uploads/file) move binary files with signed intents and session-revocation re-checks (uploads/file/route.ts:15-25); the AI cannot provide bytes, so these are not automation candidates. The service desk accepts base64 photos inline (service-desk/service.ts:100-110). /api/media/[...key] is GET-only streaming (logos public, profile images same-school only) and has no write handler.
- Unsupported DELETE handlers (static 405, no capability): front-desk visitors (route.ts:102), postal (route.ts:80), complaints (route.ts:111), phone-calls (route.ts:81). These records are corrected or closed, never erased.
- Related routes outside this cluster's directories that use these services: /api/family/trips (guardian consent answer, family/trips.ts:answerTrip :180, PARENT only), /api/family/transport (guardian journey view), /api/cron/service-desk and /api/cron/integrations (system jobs), /api/public/v1/* (API-key-authenticated external roster API, integrations/service.ts:authenticate :256, linkExternalId :314). None of these are AI-user capabilities.
- SUPER_ADMIN edge cases: the lending desk needs user.campusId (desk.ts:30), so a SUPER_ADMIN without a pinned campus gets 400; certificate services exclude SUPER_ADMIN even though the route matrix passes them (certificates/service.ts:31,40); the front_desk.log_call and library.create_books adapters exclude SUPER_ADMIN although the routes allow it (role-action-service.ts:17,41). Whether SUPER_ADMIN sessions normally carry a campusId: unknown.
- Onboarding questionnaire relevance: these domains hold setup data that can be generated safely through existing services: library rules (desk.ts:saveRules) and categories; transport vehicles, routes and stops (vehicleSchema/transportRouteSchema plus saveStop/setRouteVehicles); dormitory room types and rooms; service-desk categories (createCategory, which needs real staff ids for owner and escalation); certificate template drafts (which still need a CAMPUS_ADMIN approval); data retention schedules (authority only). None of these have typed adapters today.

**Read lookups the AI needs for name→id resolution (33):** `GET /api/owner/schools?search=&status=&plan=&page=&limit=` (Resolve a school name, slug or contact email to schoolId (pa); `GET /api/owner/schools/[id]` (A school's campuses (ids, names, counts) and its SUPER_ADMIN); `GET /api/owner/users?search=&role=&schoolId=&status=` (Resolve a person (name/email/phone) to userId across schools); `GET /api/owner/sessions?userId=&active=` (Find loginSession ids to terminate); `GET /api/owner/support/grants` (Support grant and incident ids and their status); `GET /api/owner/support/session` (The currently active support grant (needs the cookie)); `GET /api/owner/support/workspace?grantId=&domain=` (Audited, scope-limited reads of a supported school); `GET /api/owner/subscription-requests` (Pending subscription request ids (PENDING_REVIEW, oldest fir); `GET /api/owner/provider-events` (Billing provider event ids that need review); `GET /api/owner/pilots` (Pilot ids, eligible measurement owners (APP_OWNER users) and); `GET /api/owner/pilots/[id]` (Observation, measure and claim ids with versions for one pil); `GET /api/owner/capacity` (Current policies (ids), telemetry and recorded runs); `GET /api/owner/workflows?schoolId=&eventId=` (Reconcile and list a school's outbox workflows (event ids, s); `GET /api/owner/platform-config` (Current default plan pricing); `GET /api/owner/payment-settings` (Payment gateway and bank configuration state); `GET /api/owner/audit-logs?action=&days=` (Platform operator audit trail); `GET /api/owner/stats` (Platform totals); `GET /api/owner/pilots/export` (Approved, non-stale pilot claims export); `GET /api/super/users?search=&role=&campusId=&status=` (Resolve a staff name or email to userId within own school); `GET /api/super/sessions?userId=&active=` (Login session ids within own school); `GET /api/super/support-grants` (Pending, active or emergency support grant ids to review); `GET /api/super/audit-logs, GET /api/super/stats` (Institution audit trail and totals); `GET /api/onboarding/package` (Plan catalogue, limits, checkout mode and payment methods fo); `GET /api/onboarding/package/status?intentId=` (State of a checkout intent); `GET /api/readiness` (Readiness checks, version, task ids and the staff list (owne); `GET /api/navigation/access` (The caller's effective module and capability booleans (catal); `GET /api/navigation/counts` (Approval badge counts (platform approvals for APP_OWNER)); `GET /api/search?q=&types=student,class,staff,application&campus=&year=&class=&status= (and ?options=1)` (Resolve student, class, staff or application names to ids; o); `GET /api/offline?view=prepare&classId=&date= | review | capabilities` (Register snapshot with entry versions, and the leader review); `GET /api/jobs, GET /api/jobs/[id]` (Background job ids and item ids for retry or cancel); `GET /api/audit-log, GET /api/audit-log/export` (Scoped audit history for a campus or the group); `GET /api/public/v1/students, GET /api/public/v1/students/[id], GET /api/public/v1/contract` (Integration roster reads (roster.read API key) and the publi); `GET /api/public/admissions?form= | ?reference= (X-Resume-Key)` (A published admission form, or the family's own application)

**UI-only / server-action writes (no API route):**
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:saveOnboardingStep — save or complete one institution-setup step (setup-service.ts:247 saveSetup); SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN creator or delegate; no API route
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:finishOnboarding — activate the setup: campuses, cycles, weekends, role/campus changes, then re-issue the session (setup-service.ts:330 completeSetup); no API route
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:assignOnboardingDelegate — creator names a setup delegate; creates an ADMIN or SUPER_ADMIN account and returns a temporaryPassword (setup-service.ts:517); no API route
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:revokeOnboardingDelegate — deactivates the delegate and ends their sessions (setup-service.ts:562); no API route
- src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:getOnboardingSession — read the setup view (setup-service.ts:164 setupView)
- src/app/actions/addCampus.ts:addCampus — group admin opens a campus (isGroupConsole), seeds its calendar and optionally invites a CAMPUS_ADMIN; the automation campus.create tool covers it only partially and without calendar seeding
- src/app/actions/settings.ts:updateSchoolDetails — school identity edit by the institution authority (settings.ts:91-94) with a revision check and audit; no API route
- src/app/actions/settings.ts:updateCampusDetails — campus edit: SUPER_ADMIN for any campus, CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN for their own (settings.ts:97-107); no API route
- src/app/actions/settings.ts:previewCampusArchive / archiveCampus / restoreCampus — group admin only (isGroupConsole, settings.ts:405-407); archive is blocked by active students, classes, open invoices, pending payments or the last active campus; no API route
- src/app/actions/settings.ts:getInstitutionHistory — read the school or campus change history
- src/app/actions/completeTeacherOnboarding.ts:completeTeacherOnboarding — teacher first-run profile (TEACHER only, onboardingComplete false); automation profile.update overlaps only on qualification, specialization, subjectSpecialties and experience
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:saveLanguagePreference — own preferredLanguage, overlapping the automation profile.update tool
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:previewLocaleChange / applyLocaleChange — school or campus locale policy with a signed preview token, delegated-field checks and an exam-calendar lock
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:setLocaleDelegation — delegate locale fields to a campus
- src/app/actions/locale.ts:reviewLocaleCurrency — ACCOUNTANT checker approves a currency change made by someone else (createdBy not self)
- src/app/actions/invite.ts:inviteStaff / removeStaff / cancelInvitation / resendInvitation — membershipAuthority (src/lib/membership-access.ts:68); an API equivalent for invitations exists at src/app/api/staff/route.ts, outside this cluster
- src/app/actions/invite.ts:acceptInvite / acceptInviteResult — invitee sets a password and joins (credential flow)

**Domain invariants an adapter must preserve:**
- Automation coverage for this cluster is effectively zero. None of the 15 school.execute operations (src/lib/school-ai/operations.ts:2; execution.ts:34-49) touch platform, governance or onboarding routes. The only overlapping catalog tools are platform.inspect / platform.update_institution (APP_OWNER, catalog.ts:29) and campus.create (SUPER_ADMIN, catalog.ts:32). All three are inline prisma in src/lib/automation/platform.ts:10-27 and role-action-service.ts:29-34 and call no route service.
- platform.update_institution writes school name, city and website directly (automation/platform.ts:18-27), but no owner API route edits those fields. Owner PATCH /api/owner/schools/[id] handles only plan and status (route.ts:56-91). The support-grant write path allows only phone, contactEmail and website (support-access.ts:48). The tool also skips logSuperAdminAction and the support-session refusal that requirePlatformOwner applies (scope.ts:92-93). For APP_OWNER, automation authorize skips assertSchoolOperational and the 'ai' permission (automation/service.ts:33).
- campus.create diverges from the UI server action addCampus (src/app/actions/addCampus.ts:35). (a) The role check is user.role==='SUPER_ADMIN' (role-action-service.ts:30) instead of isGroupConsole (addCampus.ts:43; institution.ts:32). (b) seedCampusCalendar is not called (addCampus.ts:104,126-169), so a new campus gets no ACTIVE academicCycle or weekend rows. (c) There is no email, phone or website validation (addCampus.ts:50-53). (d) regId is a hash 'C-…' instead of 'BR-…'. (e) The optional CAMPUS_ADMIN invite is not sent (addCampus.ts:108). An adapter should call the same logic as addCampus and keep assertPlanCapacity(campuses) inside the transaction (role-action-service.ts:31).
- Feature 2 (first-onboarding questionnaire): the institution setup wizard has NO API route. It exists only as the server actions saveOnboardingStep, finishOnboarding, assignOnboardingDelegate and revokeOnboardingDelegate (src/app/actions/completeOnboarding.ts:81-171). These call src/lib/onboarding/setup-service.ts saveSetup(user,input):247, completeSetup(user,expectedVersion):330, assignSetupDelegate:517 and revokeSetupDelegate:562, which are clean (AuthUser, input) functions. Every call must run inside runWithTenantContext(setupTenantContext(user)) (completeOnboarding.ts:46; setup-service.ts:49-51).
- Setup actor rule: the role must be SUPER_ADMIN or ADMIN, and the actor is either the creator (isInstitutionOwner and school.contactEmail === user.email) or the named delegate (setup-service.ts:137-149). Every other role is refused with AccessDenied.
- Setup concurrency: saves are versioned (expectedVersion, setup-service.ts:251; updateMany version guard :270-283). Completion claims the row with a status/version updateMany in one transaction (setup-service.ts:347-353). Re-running a COMPLETED setup replays the stored result (setup-service.ts:334), so completeSetup is safe to retry. A failed run records failedStep and lastError so the wizard can resume.
- Setup steps: structure (adoptionMode EXAM_STUDIO|FULL_OPERATIONS), identity, package, campuses (GROUP only), academic and review (src/lib/onboarding/setup-state.ts:13-27). Validation rules are in validateStep (setup-state.ts:136-187). The draft schema is school + up to 50 campus drafts (setup-state.ts:42-84). This is the natural typed input for an AI-generated setup.
- Setup completion side effects: it activates campuses only up to the plan's maxCampuses and keeps the overflow as drafts (splitByCapacity, setup-service.ts:365). It creates an ACTIVE academicCycle and weekend rows for each campus. It sets the owner/delegate role to SUPER_ADMIN (GROUP) or ADMIN (STANDALONE) with the campus pinned for STANDALONE, and bumps accessVersion on a role change (:449). It clears canPurchaseSubscription outside the authority role (:456). After this the session must be re-issued (completeOnboarding.ts:99,109-145). The automation engine rejects a job whose accessVersion is stale (automation/service.ts:32), so a questionnaire-driven job must expect to stop and resume after completion.
- The existing automation 'questionnaire' is a different object: a school-ai ONBOARDING workspace record limited to ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN, scoped to one campus, with only confirmedKeys passed to the model (automation/service.ts:43-48; school-ai/schemas.ts:25-35). It is not the InstitutionSetup wizard. Setup-task delegation for those facts lives in src/app/api/school-ai/setup-tasks (outside this cluster), gated by isReadinessLead (school-ai/access.ts:317-318).
- Onboarding package purchase: assertSetupPurchaser requires onboardingComplete=false, and a delegate also needs canPurchaseSubscription (setup-service.ts:151-156). The POST requires an idempotencyKey that is bound to the selection (checkout-intents.ts:17,80-93). The plan never changes before settlement. The AI may at most record a FREE or pay-later choice (onboarding/package/route.ts:184-190); a real checkout returns a provider URL that a human must complete.
- Support-access separation of duties: the APP_OWNER requests with MFA (support/grants/route.ts:25). The target school's institution authority approves, rejects or revokes with MFA (super/support-grants/route.ts:27-46). Emergency grants (60 min or less) are active immediately and need post-hoc evidence review (support/grants/route.ts:34,49-50; super/support-grants/route.ts:47-56). The session is an HttpOnly cookie (support/grants/[id]/start/route.ts:28-30; support-access.ts:72-90), so a server-side adapter cannot drive it. The restricted scopes safeguarding, clinic, counselling and custody can never be granted (support-access.ts:10).
- Credentials: /api/owner/schools POST and /api/owner/users POST return a one-time tempPassword (schools/route.ts:179,269; users/route.ts:133,182). Both password-reset routes accept a plaintext newPassword. An AI adapter must never generate, relay or type credentials. Note that the owner reset has no MFA and no target restriction (owner/users/[id]/password/route.ts:12-29), while the institution reset requires MFA and forbids self, authority and APP_OWNER targets (super/users/[id]/password/route.ts:19-44).
- Seat and capacity invariants: owner user provisioning meters only TEACHER seats, counting pending invitations (provisioning.ts:97-121). Roles are limited to OWNER_CREATABLE_ROLES (provisioning.ts:17-23). Every non-SUPER_ADMIN must be pinned to a campus of the target school (owner/users/route.ts:120-129). Campus creation uses assertPlanCapacity metric 'campuses' (role-action-service.ts:31; addCampus.ts:59).
- School lifecycle: offboarding is a soft status=DELETED with an exact typed-name confirmation, and purge is refused (owner/schools/[id]/route.ts:141,153). Restoring with ACTIVE clears the grace state and the tombstone (:83-86). A plan change rewrites commercialContract and aiCreditsLimit (:66-72). Subscription-request approvals change the school state only for GRACE_EXTENSION and CANCELLATION (owner/subscription-requests/route.ts:47-55).
- Audit gaps an adapter must close: platform-config PUT and payment-settings POST write no audit row (platform-config/route.ts:48-52; payment-settings/route.ts:57-61,86-90). The owner routes' logSuperAdminAction calls are fire-and-forget with .catch(() => {}). super/sessions DELETE writes no audit (route.ts:52-58). payment-settings save-bank stores the account number and IBAN in platformConfig JSON and must remain human-only.
- /api/owner/workflows POST authenticates with getAuthUser plus requireWorkflowOperator, a DB check for role='APP_OWNER' (queue/operations.ts:5-9), not requirePlatformOwner. A retry is refused while an external effect is 'uncertain' (operations.ts:17-18). Reasons are fixed enums (queue/operation-contract.ts:3-4).
- Jobs: authorizeJob re-reads the live account (role, campus, accessVersion, active, school not DELETED), the module edit flag and the teacher's class assignment under a FOR UPDATE lock (jobs/service.ts:55-91,280-283). Adapters must pass the real AuthUser with its accessVersion.
- Readiness: the lead is the institution authority or the named setup delegate (readiness/service.ts:22-26). Approval re-evaluates the checks inside a serializable schoolAiTransaction, with a version guard (:107-121). A rehearse call bumps the version (:175).
- Cron routes are machine-only (CRON_SECRET bearer) and should never be AI tools. risk-digest is the exception to the production fail-closed pattern: it checks only if CRON_SECRET is set (cron/risk-digest/route.ts:16, compare group-policy/route.ts:14). It has no dedupe, so every run consumes AI credits.
- Pilots: reviewClaim has a version guard but no check that the proposer and the reviewer are different people (pilots/service.ts:207-238). An adapter that wants separation of duties must add it.
- /api/tenants POST is a legacy provisioning path. It creates a school with no campus and no owner, defaults the kind to GROUP, creates a per-tenant DB schema and seeds role permissions, and these steps are not in one transaction (tenants/route.ts:15,52-72). The full provisioning path is /api/owner/schools POST.
- Directory facts: src/app/api/pilots does not exist; the pilot routes are under src/app/api/owner/pilots. src/lib/school holds only the country.ts and details.ts validators, which addCampus and settings reuse. Governance-adjacent API dirs outside this cluster: src/app/api/group-policy, memberships, roles, invite, staff, billing, campus-packages, school-ai/setup-tasks.
- ROLE_SETUP_INTERVIEW already says that APP_OWNER provisioning, subscriptions and support access go through owner-console flows, and that SUPER_ADMIN campus-admin appointment goes through access management (src/lib/automation/role-setup.ts:3-4). Any new adapters must keep those boundaries.

---

# Appendix A supplement — C9 school-ai, ai and automation routes (added after the completeness critic)


**C9 totals:** 154 additional write capabilities; AI today: {'none': 143, 'partial': 8, 'full': 3}.


## C9a school-ai exams and Exam Studio — 94 write capabilities

_Verifier: The mutation handlers are complete. I found 68 route.ts files across the 13 listed dirs and the 7 unlisted ones (capabilities, department-approvals, family-notes, paper-slots, pilot-ledger, studio, teaching-progress). Every POST/PATCH/PUT branch has a row, including every action-enum branch (moderation 5, repairs 4, results 4, scripts 4, item-evidence 3, practice-sessions 7, capture 2, learning 2, next-lesson 2, family-notes 2) and the body-shape branches (clarifications, form-sets). No mutating handler is missing. The gaps are the GET handlers, which the inventory deliberately left out. I added 15 READ rows for them, prioritising those an inspect adapter needs before acting. I spot-checked 17 rows; most hold, with these errors. (1) Wrong roles/gate: the 'gate anomaly' for next-lesson and exams/[id]/practice is false. scopedAssignment calls staffExamAccess(view) for non-students (exam-service.ts:86), so only managers and TEACHER get through, not ACCOUNTANT, LIBRARIAN or RECEPTIONIST. (2) Wrong idempotency: extend is idempotent through idempotencyKey and an extensionKey audit check (control-room-service.ts:56,70-71). next-lesson confirm and practice draft are not idempotent (randomUUID keys). (3) Wrong input schema: accommodation approve uses approveExamAccommodationSchema (exam.ts:44) with a version lock. (4) Confirmed: written-marking sends the raw student response through generateAIDraft with no pseudonymizer (written-marking-service.ts:7,16-30), which breaks the PII policy. Automation coverage really is none, apart from exam-session-plan options feeding exams.create_session_plan (execution.ts:44). Minor line drift: updatePilotLedger is at :66 and extendAttempts at :57._

**Domain notes:**
- Automation coverage is zero for this cluster: src/lib/automation/catalog.ts:29-45 exposes no exam/paper/practice tools, and the only exam op in src/lib/school-ai/execution.ts:44 is exams.create_session_plan (WORKFLOW, exams.view+add+edit,students.view), reached via school.execute and held by ACADEMIC_TOOLS (access.ts:59) for PRINCIPAL and seat-vacant CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN. exam-session-plan/options/route.ts:5 is GET-only feed for that op.
- Routes are thin: almost all use requireAuthUser (src/lib/api/scope.ts) and do the real gate in the service, so typed adapters can call the service functions directly with (user, id, raw). Exceptions needing wrappers: attempt start/save (withSebEvidence + measured CRITICAL), generate-paper (rate limit + streaming), scan (multipart), capture (QR), proctor clip upload (binary).
- Core staff gate is staffExamAccess (exam-service.ts:32): live actor/accessVersion re-check, ai.<action> + exams.<action> grants, manager or TEACHER; teacher limited to own sitting, assigned subject, or (non-edit) on-duty invigilator (exam-service.ts:41-46). resultDecisionAccess (exam-service.ts:59) lets a department-head teacher decide.
- Exam Studio role matrix is src/lib/school-ai/studio-access.ts:89-127 (STUDIO_AUTHORITY) with grants at :159 and role derivation at :~185: AUTHOR=manager|TEACHER in ASSIGNED_SUBJECT; ACADEMIC_APPROVER=isSchoolAiManager (SUPER_ADMIN,CAMPUS_ADMIN,ADMIN,PRINCIPAL); CANDIDATE=STUDENT; GUARDIAN=PARENT; PLATFORM_SUPPORT=APP_OWNER. EDIT_RELEASED_FORM and DELETE_SIGNAL are held by no role (sealing/immutability). Studio-gated mutations: RELEASE_RESULT, AUTHORIZE_REMEDY, APPROVE_VERSION, AUTHOR_ITEM, EXPORT_EVIDENCE_PACK; each records school_ai_studio_actions audit.
- Separation of duties enforced in code: correction proposer cannot decide (exam-moderation-service.ts:91); second marker must be assigned reviewer (:168) and cannot resolve own discrepancy (:204); repair proposer cannot approve and approver must be manager (assessment-repair.ts:229-233); workspace approvals use assertApprovalSeparation (access.ts:191). An AI adapter must carry the real human actor so these checks still bite and must never act as both proposer and approver.
- Release/sealing invariants: results release requires window closed and no SUBMITTED attempts (exam-release-service.ts:268-270); remedies refused after release, must go through correction proposals (exam-incident-service.ts:184); form set assign requires all forms APPROVED and parity, then seals (form-set-service.ts:231,255); proctor policy frozen after first attempt (exam-proctor-service.ts:46); extra time refused after start (exam-service.ts:269); family notes only after release and report-card gate OPEN (family-note-service.ts:48,121).
- Candidate-only operations (attempt start/save/submit, integrity events, proctor start/clips/end, clarification acknowledge, appeals, practice start/hint/answer/finish) should be excluded from AI automation or restricted to non-answering actions (reschedule revisit, file appeal), to protect academic integrity. A study assistant already blocks help during an active exam (src/lib/study/assistant.ts:~58).
- AI credits: written marks (written-marking-service.ts:31), script suggestions (script-response-service.ts:36), language comparison 1 credit (form-set-service.ts:328), learning lesson 1 / semantic 2 (learning-service.ts:30), paper generation reserve+reconcile (paper-generation-service.ts:64,126). Credits are charged only on valid output; idempotency via aIUsageLog idempotencyKey or requestHash. An automation layer calling these would double-meter if it also charges its own planning turn.
- PII: script-response-service.ts:9 uses Pseudonymizer/scanForPII and paper-generation handles PIILeakError, but no pseudonymize import was seen in written-marking-service.ts, learning-service.ts or form-set-service.ts. Check these against the pseudonymize-before-prompt policy before exposing them to automation. Evidence packs, proctor clips and integrity events carry sensitive candidate data with retentionUntil.
- Gate anomalies to verify: next-lesson propose/confirm and exams/[id]/practice only use scopedAssignment plus a non-student check (next-lesson-service.ts:25, practice-service.ts:34), with no staffExamAccess, so any campus staff role (ACCOUNTANT, LIBRARIAN, RECEPTIONIST) may reach them. teacherRevisit writes behind view-only grants (practice-session-service.ts:225-229). generate-paper/scan/capture admit all 8 SCHOOL_AI_STAFF_ROLES via requireSchoolAiUser (access.ts:13,122).
- Unlisted subdirs found and included: capabilities (GET only), department-approvals, family-notes, paper-slots (brief POST; mine GET), pilot-ledger, studio/next-step (GET), teaching-progress (line POST; GET). GET-only routes (board, compare, formative-comparison, improvement, pulse, twin, verify, print, seb-config, evidence-pack download, proctor clip read, question-bank list, practice, secure-profiles seb) are READ candidates for a school.inspect-style adapter but have no rows here.

## C9b school-ai workspace, setup, runs, assistant, plus /api/ai and /api/automatio — 61 write capabilities

_Verifier: Mostly complete for the dirs named. I enumerated every route.ts under src/app/api/school-ai, src/app/api/ai and src/app/api/automation with grep for exported handlers. Every non-GET handler in the 20 named dirs has an inventory row. lesson-plans POST has exactly 4 actions (lesson-plans/route.ts:24); setup/generate GET is read-only (route.ts:14-21); workspace/[id]/execute GET is a preview.

The inventory extended into 7 unlisted subdirs but left out two intake-adjacent mutating POSTs: school-ai/capture (mints a signed capture link, 15-minute JWT) and school-ai/scan (builds a PDF from page images). Both are added above.

Other unlisted subdirs with mutating handlers are not in these rows and must belong to another cluster (presumably C9a Exam Studio), or they are untracked:
- exams/* (around 30 routes, including proctor, integrity PUT, results-release, gradebook, moderation, remedies apply, accommodations)
- form-sets/*
- generate-paper (+ stream)
- question-bank/reuse
- practice-sessions
- secure-profiles (+ [id] PATCH)
- item-evidence
- learning POST
- figures POST

Spot-checks (12):
- **Confirmed:**
  - SCHOOL_AI_STAFF_ROLES and manager set (access.ts:13-16).
  - allowedWorkspaceKinds per role (access.ts:35-39).
  - Op allow-list, with SUPER_ADMIN getting an empty set (access.ts:72-77).
  - assertApprovalRole lets ACCOUNTANT through for the finance ops only (access.ts:155-157).
  - Separation of duties checks both createdById and updatedById (access.ts:184-188).
  - Execute needs APPROVED + approvedById at the exact version (execution.ts:157) and is idempotent on requestedById+idempotencyKey (execution.ts:247).
  - workspace.create is idempotent (repository.ts:341).
  - Automation self-approves the record before preview and execute (automation/service.ts:81). The questionnaire is ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN only (:45).
  - The automation route really uses requireAutomaticUser, aliased as requireAuthUser (automation/route.ts:1, auth.ts:2-5).
  - Intake idempotency key is a content hash (intake/route.ts:46). Transcribe charges once per key (transcribe/route.ts:31-34).
  - Pilot-ledger opt-out deletes all snapshots (pilot-ledger-service.ts:72).
  - lesson-plans assertOwner is owner OR canManageOperations (lesson-plan-service.ts:40-43).
  - canGenerateRemarks / canTranslate role sets (generate-remarks:26-28, translate:18-20).
  - canConfigureCampus is ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN (setup-role.ts).
- **Wrong:**
  - runs pause/cancel are campus-scoped, not actor-owned; only resume and advance need the original actor.
  - SUPER_ADMIN cannot create, pause, cancel or advance runs, because each step goes back through execution authorize.
  - Several rows say yes or unknown on idempotency but use only version compare-and-swap, which makes them non-idempotent (409 on replay): setup-tasks PATCH, runs PATCH, document-job PATCH.
  - Some automation line refs are off by 1-3 lines._

**Domain notes:**
- GET-only routes in cluster (no rows): workspace GET list/[id], workspace/[id]/execute GET preview (execute/route.ts:14), workspace/[id]/announcements, print, dna, brief GET, rehearsal GET readiness; setup/journey, setup/generate GET; setup-tasks GET, setup-tasks/options, setup-tasks/[id] GET; runs GET; imports/options (requires assertApprovalRole + roster add, imports/options/route.ts:11); sources/[id]/file, sources/[id]/jobs GET; conversations GET/[id] GET (+?export=1 JSON download); assistant/catalog (ai.add); operations/options, risks, lifecycle-options, publication-options; timetable/options; finance-operations/options; fee-invoices/options; announcements/options; school-setup/options; insights GET; capabilities GET; paper-slots/mine; studio/next-step; teaching-progress GET; lesson-plans GET; department-approvals GET; pilot-ledger GET; /api/ai/credits GET; /api/ai/insights GET; /api/automation GET.
- Gate resolution: requireSchoolAiUser(action) = requireAuthUser (scope.ts:42) + SCHOOL_AI_STAFF_ROLES (access.ts:13: SUPER_ADMIN, CAMPUS_ADMIN, ADMIN, PRINCIPAL, TEACHER, ACCOUNTANT, LIBRARIAN, RECEPTIONIST) + campus assignment for non-SA (access.ts:49) + ai.<action> matrix (SUPER_ADMIN bypasses matrix, access.ts:113). Managers = isSchoolAiManager (access.ts:14). canConfigureCampus = ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN (setup-role.ts:2). canManageOperations/canManageAcademics = SA, campus admins, PRINCIPAL (scope.ts:218-226).
- Typed-op allow-list (access.ts:56-77): PRINCIPAL = 7 academic tools + record_risk_decision + queue_announcement; ACCOUNTANT = finance.apply_operation + fees.generate_invoices; CAMPUS_ADMIN/ADMIN = 6 operator tools + finance tools only when campus has no accountant + academic tools only when no principal (seats via getCampusSeats, access.ts:84); SUPER_ADMIN/TEACHER/LIBRARIAN/RECEPTIONIST execute nothing. Same allow-list feeds UI capabilities and execution.authorize (execution.ts:137), and automation catalog school.execute (catalog.ts:44).
- Approvals: every typed op requires APPROVED status at exact version with approvedById plus matching previewHash (execution.ts:157, 52-57). assertApprovalRole (access.ts:155) = managers, plus ACCOUNTANT for finance WORKFLOWs. Exam-chain kinds use mayApprove (HoD / academic lead; term papers need seal authority, repository.ts:447-475). Separation of duties (access.ts:184-200) checks BOTH createdById and updatedById against Delivery_Policy; platform default NOT_REQUIRED.
- Automation engine risk: automation/service.ts:79-84 creates a WORKFLOW record and immediately self-approves it (updateWorkspaceRecord action approve) before preview+execute; the comment treats the user's request as authorization. This collapses author==approver unless a school Delivery_Policy requires separation, and bypasses the human review step the workspace path enforces. Any new adapter layer should keep an explicit human confirm of the pending action (job.pending is surfaced, executed on next advance, service.ts:106-112).
- Automation catalog (src/lib/automation/catalog.ts) tools: profile.update, personal.save_plan (all), platform.inspect/update_institution (APP_OWNER), students.create (ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN with students.add), campus.create (SUPER_ADMIN), library.create_books, front_desk.log_call, work.save, school.inspect, school.execute (15 typed ops), curriculum.create. Not covered: workspace save/review/archive of non-WORKFLOW kinds, runs scheduling, setup tasks delegation, document jobs, conversations, lesson plans, Exam Studio workspace subroutes, teaching line, family notes, pilot ledger, department approvals, legacy /api/ai remarks/insights/translate/review.
- Sealing/release: reviewItem refuses APPROVED/ARCHIVED versions (exam-release-service.ts:53); EDIT_RELEASED_FORM and DELETE_SIGNAL held by no role (studio-access.ts:85); family notes send only after report card published and unchanged result version (family-note-service.ts:120-122); pinned items cannot be regenerated (item-regeneration-service.ts:54).
- Destructive: DELETE_ORIGINAL document job permanently removes stored source file, requires ai.delete + manager + ARCHIVED record at same version + retention check (document-jobs-service.ts:22,75,95); conversation DELETE hard-deletes messages; pilot-ledger opt-out deletes all snapshots (pilot-ledger-service.ts:72); workspace archive requires ai.delete.
- Credits: ensureAICreditsAvailable/consumeAICreditAndLog in assistant (assistant/route.ts:62,117,122), transcribe (charged once per idempotencyKey, transcribe/route.ts:31-34), setup generate/actions/analyze services, syllabus outline propose, document jobs EXTRACT, form-set, regeneration, automation (service.ts:121,168), legacy /api/ai remarks (per student in batch), insights, translate. assertSchoolOperational (billing) gates execution, runs, document jobs and automation; /api/ai uses billingAccessResponse.
- PII: legacy /api/ai generate-remarks/insights send student marks and names to the model; memory policy requires pseudonymization before prompts. Automation catalog context loads profile, class lists, teacher names (catalog.ts:36,49). Intake stores originals in private storage (sourceStorageAvailable). Students.create saves guardian email as contact only, never invites.
- Idempotency patterns: idempotencyKey on workspace create, assistant, conversations create, setup task create, runs create, execute, automation start, regenerate (uuid), transcribe; intake uses content hash; workspace PATCH/runs PATCH/conversation PATCH use optimistic version CAS (409 on replay) rather than keys.
- Worker re-resolution: queued runs and document jobs re-resolve the stored actor via resolveWorkerPrincipal and block on role/accessVersion change (access.ts:97-103); automation authorize rechecks live actor role/campus/accessVersion each call (automation/service.ts:31-32).
